`app/lib/features/visit/state/visit_commands.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../customer/state/customer_list_controller.dart';
import '../../customer/state/customer_providers.dart';
import '../../shop/state/shop_errors.dart';
import '../api/visit_api.dart';
import '../api/visit_models.dart';
import 'visit_providers.dart';

/// 방문·일정·메뉴 쓰기. 모든 호출은 매장 범위 공통 처리(`NOT_A_MEMBER` →
/// 내 정보 재조회)를 거친다.
///
/// 방문 쓰기는 **성공하든 실패하든** 하루 목록·월 요약·상세·시간 칸을 다시
/// 불러오게 한다 — 낙관적 갱신을 하지 않고, 409 가 나면 화면이 서버와 어긋나
/// 있다는 뜻이라 새로 읽어야 한다(설계 9.2). 고객 쪽(목록의 최근 방문순, 고객
/// 상세의 노쇼 횟수·다가오는 예약)도 방문에 따라 바뀌므로 함께 다시 부른다.
class VisitCommands {
  VisitCommands(this._ref);

  final Ref _ref;

  VisitApi get _api => _ref.read(visitApiProvider);

  Future<SavedVisits> register(int shopId, VisitRequest request) =>
      _visitWrite(() => _api.register(shopId, request));

  Future<SavedVisits> update(int shopId, int visitId, VisitUpdate update) =>
      _visitWrite(() => _api.update(shopId, visitId, update));

  Future<SavedVisits> reschedule(
    int shopId, {
    required List<int> visitIds,
    required String startsAt,
  }) => _visitWrite(
    () => _api.reschedule(shopId, visitIds: visitIds, startsAt: startsAt),
  );

  Future<List<Visit>> transition(
    int shopId, {
    required List<int> visitIds,
    required String action,
    String? cancelReason,
  }) => _visitWrite(
    () => _api.transition(
      shopId,
      visitIds: visitIds,
      action: action,
      cancelReason: cancelReason,
    ),
  );

  /// "지난번처럼" — 반려동물을 고를 때 한 번 읽는다(다시 부르지 않는다).
  Future<VisitDefaults> defaults(int shopId, int petId) =>
      runShopScoped(_ref, () => _api.defaults(shopId, petId));

  /// 다음 예약 초안(저장하지 않는다 — 읽기라 아무것도 다시 부르지 않는다).
  Future<NextVisitDraft> nextDraft(int shopId, int visitId) =>
      runShopScoped(_ref, () => _api.nextDraft(shopId, visitId));

  Future<VisitSettings> updateSettings(int shopId, VisitSettings settings) =>
      _scheduleWrite(() => _api.updateSettings(shopId, settings));

  Future<ClosedDate> addClosedDate(int shopId, DateTime date, {String? note}) =>
      _scheduleWrite(() => _api.addClosedDate(shopId, date, note: note));

  Future<void> deleteClosedDate(int shopId, DateTime date) =>
      _scheduleWrite(() => _api.deleteClosedDate(shopId, date));

  Future<Menu> createMenu(
    int shopId, {
    required String name,
    int? defaultDurationMinutes,
  }) => _menuWrite(
    () => _api.createMenu(
      shopId,
      name: name,
      defaultDurationMinutes: defaultDurationMinutes,
    ),
  );

  Future<Menu> updateMenu(
    int shopId,
    int menuId, {
    required String name,
    required int? defaultDurationMinutes,
    required int sortOrder,
  }) => _menuWrite(
    () => _api.updateMenu(
      shopId,
      menuId,
      name: name,
      defaultDurationMinutes: defaultDurationMinutes,
      sortOrder: sortOrder,
    ),
  );

  Future<Menu> archiveMenu(int shopId, int menuId) =>
      _menuWrite(() => _api.archiveMenu(shopId, menuId));

  Future<T> _visitWrite<T>(Future<T> Function() action) async {
    try {
      return await runShopScoped(_ref, action);
    } finally {
      _ref.invalidate(dayVisitsProvider);
      _ref.invalidate(calendarMonthProvider);
      _ref.invalidate(visitDetailProvider);
      _ref.invalidate(visitSlotsProvider);
      _ref.invalidate(customerListControllerProvider);
      _ref.invalidate(customerDetailProvider);
    }
  }

  /// 영업시간·쉬는 요일·임시 휴무는 시간 칸의 경고와 달력·하루의 휴무 표시를 바꾼다.
  Future<T> _scheduleWrite<T>(Future<T> Function() action) async {
    final result = await runShopScoped(_ref, action);
    _ref.invalidate(visitSettingsProvider);
    _ref.invalidate(closedDatesProvider);
    _ref.invalidate(visitSlotsProvider);
    _ref.invalidate(dayVisitsProvider);
    _ref.invalidate(calendarMonthProvider);
    return result;
  }

  Future<T> _menuWrite<T>(Future<T> Function() action) async {
    final result = await runShopScoped(_ref, action);
    _ref.invalidate(menusProvider);
    return result;
  }
}

final visitCommandsProvider = Provider<VisitCommands>(
  (ref) => VisitCommands(ref),
);
```

`app/lib/features/visit/state/visit_providers.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../../core/proxy/providers.dart';
import '../../shop/state/shop_errors.dart';
import '../api/visit_api.dart';
import '../api/visit_models.dart';

final visitApiProvider = Provider<VisitApi>(
  (ref) => VisitApi(ref.watch(apiClientProvider)),
);

typedef DayKey = ({int shopId, DateTime date});
typedef MonthKey = ({int shopId, DateTime month});
typedef VisitKey = ({int shopId, int visitId});
typedef MenusKey = ({int shopId, bool includeArchived});

/// 시간 칸 조회 조건. [exclude]는 옮길 방문 ID 를 쉼표로 이은 문자열이다 —
/// 목록은 값으로 비교되지 않아 family 키가 매번 달라진다.
typedef SlotsKey = ({
  int shopId,
  DateTime date,
  int duration,
  String? at,
  String exclude,
});

/// 모든 조회는 화면을 떠나면 버리고(autoDispose) 매장 범위 공통 처리를 거친다.
/// 쓰기가 끝나면 `VisitCommands`가 다시 불러오게 한다.
final dayVisitsProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose.family<DayVisits, DayKey>(
  (ref, key) => runShopScoped(
    ref,
    () => ref.read(visitApiProvider).day(key.shopId, key.date),
  ),
);

/// 월 요약. 키의 [MonthKey.month]는 그달 1일이어야 한다([monthKeyOf]).
final calendarMonthProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose
    .family<CalendarMonth, MonthKey>(
      (ref, key) => runShopScoped(
        ref,
        () => ref.read(visitApiProvider).calendar(key.shopId, key.month),
      ),
    );

MonthKey monthKeyOf(int shopId, DateTime date) =>
    (shopId: shopId, month: DateTime(date.year, date.month));

final visitSlotsProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose
    .family<VisitSlots, SlotsKey>(
      (ref, key) => runShopScoped(
        ref,
        () => ref
            .read(visitApiProvider)
            .slots(
              key.shopId,
              date: key.date,
              durationMinutes: key.duration,
              at: key.at,
              exclude: [
                for (final id in key.exclude.split(','))
                  if (id.isNotEmpty) int.parse(id),
              ],
            ),
      ),
    );

final visitDetailProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose
    .family<VisitDetail, VisitKey>(
      (ref, key) => runShopScoped(
        ref,
        () => ref.read(visitApiProvider).detail(key.shopId, key.visitId),
      ),
    );

final visitSettingsProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose
    .family<VisitSettings, int>(
      (ref, shopId) =>
          runShopScoped(ref, () => ref.read(visitApiProvider).settings(shopId)),
    );

final menusProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose.family<List<Menu>, MenusKey>(
  (ref, key) => runShopScoped(
    ref,
    () => ref
        .read(visitApiProvider)
        .menus(key.shopId, includeArchived: key.includeArchived),
  ),
);

/// 오늘부터 1년 안의 임시 휴무.
final closedDatesProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose
    .family<List<ClosedDate>, ({int shopId, DateTime from})>(
      (ref, key) => runShopScoped(
        ref,
        () => ref
            .read(visitApiProvider)
            .closedDates(
              key.shopId,
              from: key.from,
              to: DateTime(key.from.year + 1, key.from.month, key.from.day),
            ),
      ),
    );
```

```diff
 ///
```

Expected: 12개 통과(명령 6, 보기 방식 2, 저장소 4)

Run (`app/`): `flutter analyze` → `No issues found!`, `flutter test --timeout 30s` → 전부 통과(360개).

- [ ] **Step 5: 판별력을 확인한다(변이)**

각 변이를 하나씩 넣고 그 테스트가 실패하는지 본 뒤 되돌린다.

- `_visitWrite`의 `try/finally`를 "성공한 뒤에만 다시 부른다"로 바꾼다 → `다른 기기가 먼저 바꿔 409 가 나도 다시 불러온다` 실패.
- `_scheduleWrite`에서 `dayVisitsProvider`·`calendarMonthProvider` 무효화를 뺀다 → `임시 휴무를 더하면 설정·휴무·하루·달을 다시 불러오고…` 실패.

- [ ] **Step 6: 커밋한다**

```bash
git add -A
git commit -m "feat(visit): 방문 조회 프로바이더·쓰기 명령·하루 화면 보기 방식

Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

---

### Task 4: 예약 등록 — ① 고객 찾기, ② 폼

예약 폼 `VisitForm`(등록 ②와 Task 5 의 수정이 함께 쓴다)과 등록 화면 `RegisterVisitScreen`(① 고객 검색은 고객 탭의 검색·목록 컨트롤러 재사용). 시간 칸의 줄긋기·경고는 서버 응답만 쓰고, 고른 시각은 `slots?at=`으로 한 번 더 물어 그 문자열로 저장한다(새로 정한 것 1~7). 저장하면 날짜를 돌려주며 닫는다. 테스트 도우미 `visit_test_backend.dart`(`VisitTestBackend`, `settle`, `useTallView`, `Closed`, `pushOnHome`, `bodyOf`)는 뒤 작업의 화면 테스트가 모두 쓴다. `Pressable`에 `onLongPress`를 더해 시간 칸(10분 단위)과 소요 ±(30분)의 길게 누르기에 쓴다. [지금 시작]은 늘 보인다. ①의 [새 고객 등록]은 `/customers/new?pick=1`로 열고 돌아온 고객 ID 로 ②에 바로 간다 — `RegisterCustomerScreen`에 `pickForReservation`을 더한다.

라우트 `/visits/new`(`?date=` 또는 `extra: NextVisitDraft`). `_isShopScoped`에 `/visits/` 를 더한다.

**Files:**
- Modify: `app/lib/core/router/app_router.dart`
- Modify: `app/lib/core/router/screen_keys.dart`
- Modify: `app/lib/design/widgets/pressable.dart`
- Modify: `app/lib/features/customer/screens/register_customer_screen.dart`
- Create: `app/lib/features/visit/screens/register_visit_screen.dart`
- Create: `app/lib/features/visit/screens/visit_form.dart`
- Create: `app/lib/features/visit/screens/visit_labels.dart`
- Create: `app/lib/features/visit/state/visit_form_rules.dart`
- Modify: `app/lib/l10n/app_strings.dart`
- Modify: `app/test/design/glass_boundary_test.dart`
- Modify: `app/test/features/customer/screens/register_customer_screen_test.dart`
- Create: `app/test/features/visit/screens/register_visit_screen_test.dart`
- Create: `app/test/features/visit/state/visit_form_rules_test.dart`
- Create: `app/test/support/visit_test_backend.dart`
- Golden: `app/test/features/visit/screens/goldens/register_visit_form.png` (이 작업의 골든 테스트가 `--update-goldens`로 만든다)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2·2 전부, `customerListControllerProvider`·`customerDetailProvider`·`SearchDebouncer`·`maskedTail`(customer 기능), `todayProvider`.
- Produces: `VisitForm(shopId, customerId, initial: VisitFormInitial, header, submitLabel, onSubmit, busy, errorText, onWalkIn?, singlePet, scheduleLocked, excludeVisitIds)`, `VisitFormValue{petIds, date, startsAt, durationMinutes, menuIds, memo}`, `VisitFormInitial{date, petIds, ownerOnly, at, durationMinutes, menus, memo, prefilled}`, `RegisterVisitScreen(date?, draft?)`, `ScreenKeys.registerVisit`, 라벨 도우미 `hhmm/dayLabel/sameDay/statusLabel/warningLabel/ownerLabel/visitsTitle/menusLabel`(`visit_labels.dart`), 소요 규칙 `menuDuration/stepDuration/clampDuration`(`visit_form_rules.dart`), 문구(`AppStrings` 예약 묶음).

- [ ] **Step 1: 테스트를 쓴다**

아래 테스트 파일을 그대로 만든다(수정하는 테스트는 diff 대로 고친다).

`app/test/features/visit/screens/register_visit_screen_test.dart`:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:chagocnote/core/router/app_router.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/core/router/screen_keys.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/design/widgets/app_button.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/features/home/state/today_provider.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/features/visit/api/visit_models.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/l10n/app_strings.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import '../../../support/customer_fixtures.dart';
import '../../../support/customer_test_app.dart';
import '../../../support/fake_http_client_adapter.dart';
import '../../../support/visit_fixtures.dart';
import '../../../support/visit_test_backend.dart';

final _today = [todayProvider.overrideWithValue(DateTime(2026, 9, 23))];

Future<Closed<DateTime>> _open(
  WidgetTester tester,
  CustomerTestSetup setup, {
  String path = '/visits/new?date=2026-10-02',
  Object? extra,
}) {
  useTallView(tester);
  return pushOnHome<DateTime>(tester, setup, path, extra: extra);
}

Future<void> _pickCustomer(WidgetTester tester) async {
  await tester.tap(find.text('두부').first);
  await settle(tester);
}

Future<void> _tapLabel(WidgetTester tester, String label) async {
  await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel(label).first);
  await settle(tester);
}

Finder _save() => find.byWidgetPredicate(
  (w) => w is AppButton && w.label.startsWith('예약 저장'),
);

List<String> _slotQueries(CustomerTestSetup setup) => [
  for (final r in setup.adapter.requests)
    if (r.path == '/v1/shops/3/visits/slots')
      '${r.queryParameters['date']} ${r.queryParameters['at'] ?? '-'}',
];

void main() {
  testWidgets('고객을 고르면 ②로 가고, 한 마리면 미리 골라 지난번 메뉴·소요로 채운다', (tester) async {
    final backend = VisitTestBackend();
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.registerVisit), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.reserveWhoTitle), findsOneWidget);
    await _pickCustomer(tester);

    expect(find.text(AppStrings.reserveWhenTitle), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('이수진 · ··5678'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(setup.calls, contains('GET /v1/shops/3/visits/defaults'));
    expect(
      setup.adapter.requests
          .firstWhere((r) => r.path.endsWith('/defaults'))
          .queryParameters,
      {'pet_id': 11},
    );
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.prefilledFromLast), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.durationText(90)), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.pickTimeFirst), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('시간 칸을 누르면 그 시각을 서버에 묻고, 저장은 서버가 준 시각 그대로 보내고 날짜를 돌려주며 닫는다', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final backend = VisitTestBackend();
    Map<String, dynamic>? sent;
    backend.routes['POST /v1/shops/3/visits'] = (o, body) {
      sent = bodyOf(body);
      return FakeResponse(statusCode: 201, data: savedJson([visitJson()]));
    };
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    final result = await _open(tester, setup);
    await _pickCustomer(tester);

    await _tapLabel(tester, '14:00');
    expect(_slotQueries(setup), contains('2026-10-02 14:00'));
    await tester.tap(_save());
    await settle(tester);

    expect(sent, {
      'customer_id': 7,
      'pet_ids': [11],
      'starts_at': '2026-10-02T14:00:00+09:00',
      'duration_minutes': 90,
      'menu_ids': [1],
      'memo': null,
      'walk_in': false,
    });
    expect(result.closed, isTrue);
    expect(result.value, DateTime(2026, 10, 2));
    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.registerVisit), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('겹치는 칸은 줄을 긋고, 눌러도 막지 않고 경고만 보인다', (tester) async {
    final backend = VisitTestBackend();
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits/slots'] = (o, b) {
      final at = o.queryParameters['at'] as String?;
      return FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: at == null
            ? slotsJson(
                warnings: {
                  15: ['overlap'],
                },
              )
            : slotAtJson(at: at, warnings: ['overlap']),
      );
    };
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    await _pickCustomer(tester);

    final struck = tester.widget<Text>(find.text('15:00'));
    expect(struck.style!.decoration, TextDecoration.lineThrough);
    final plain = tester.widget<Text>(find.text('14:00'));
    expect(plain.style!.decoration, isNot(TextDecoration.lineThrough));

    await _tapLabel(tester, '15:00');
    expect(find.text('· ${AppStrings.warningOverlap}'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(tester.widget<AppButton>(_save()).onPressed, isNotNull);
  });

  testWidgets('날짜를 바꾸면 그 날의 시간 칸과 고른 시각을 다시 묻는다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(VisitTestBackend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    await _pickCustomer(tester);
    await _tapLabel(tester, '14:00');

    await _tapLabel(tester, '10월 3일 토');

    expect(
      _slotQueries(setup),
      containsAll(['2026-10-03 -', '2026-10-03 14:00']),
    );
    expect(
      find.text(AppStrings.saveReservation('10월 3일 토 14:00')),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
  });

  testWidgets('다음 주로 넘기면 그 주의 날짜가 보이고, 달이 바뀌면 그달의 휴무를 읽는다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(VisitTestBackend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup, path: '/visits/new?date=2026-09-23');
    await _pickCustomer(tester);
    expect(find.bySemanticsLabel('9월 21일 월'), findsOneWidget);

    await _tapLabel(tester, AppStrings.nextWeek);

    expect(find.bySemanticsLabel('9월 28일 월'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.bySemanticsLabel('10월 4일 일'), findsOneWidget);
    final months = [
      for (final r in setup.adapter.requests)
        if (r.path.endsWith('/calendar')) r.queryParameters['month'],
    ];
    expect(months, containsAll(['2026-09', '2026-10']));
  });

  testWidgets('시간 칸을 길게 누르면 10분 단위로 고른다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(VisitTestBackend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    await _pickCustomer(tester);

    await tester.longPress(find.text('14:00'));
    await settle(tester);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.pickMinuteTitle), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text('14:40'));
    await settle(tester);

    expect(_slotQueries(setup), contains('2026-10-02 14:40'));
    expect(
      find.text(AppStrings.saveReservation('10월 2일 금 14:40')),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
  });

  testWidgets('두 마리를 고르면 한 번에 둘을, "보호자만"이면 반려동물 없이 보낸다', (tester) async {
    final backend = VisitTestBackend();
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/customers/7'] = (o, b) => FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 200,
      data: customerDetailJson(
        customer: customerJson(name: '이수진'),
        pets: [
          petJson(petId: 11, name: '두부'),
          petJson(petId: 12, name: '보리'),
          petJson(petId: 13, name: '별이', passedAwayAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z'),
        ],
      ),
    );
    final bodies = <Map<String, dynamic>>[];
    backend.routes['POST /v1/shops/3/visits'] = (o, body) {
      bodies.add(bodyOf(body));
      return const FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 422,
        data: {'code': 'VISIT_CROSSES_MIDNIGHT', 'message': 'x'},
      );
    };
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    await _pickCustomer(tester);

    // 여럿이면 미리 고르지 않는다. 무지개다리 아이는 칩이 없다.
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.pickPetFirst), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.bySemanticsLabel('별이'), findsNothing);
    // 시각을 골라도 반려동물이나 "보호자만"을 고르기 전에는 저장할 수 없다.
    await _tapLabel(tester, '14:00');
    final blocked = find.byWidgetPredicate(
      (w) => w is AppButton && w.label == AppStrings.pickPetFirst,
    );
    expect(tester.widget<AppButton>(blocked).onPressed, isNull);
    await _tapLabel(tester, '두부');
    await _tapLabel(tester, '보리');
    await tester.tap(_save());
    await settle(tester);
    expect(bodies.last['pet_ids'], [11, 12]);

    await _tapLabel(tester, AppStrings.ownerOnly);
    await tester.tap(_save());
    await settle(tester);
    expect(bodies.last['pet_ids'], isEmpty);
  });

  testWidgets('메뉴를 고르면 소요를 메뉴 합으로 채우고, 소요를 손대면 메뉴가 더는 바꾸지 않는다', (tester) async {
    final backend = VisitTestBackend();
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits/defaults'] = (o, b) => FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 200,
      data: defaultsJson(sourceVisitId: null, durationMinutes: 120, menus: []),
    );
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    await _pickCustomer(tester);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.prefilledFromLast), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.durationText(120)), findsOneWidget);

    await _tapLabel(tester, '목욕');
    await _tapLabel(tester, '부분 미용');
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.durationText(150)), findsOneWidget);

    await _tapLabel(tester, AppStrings.longer);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.durationText(160)), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.longPress(find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.longer));
    await settle(tester);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.durationText(190)), findsOneWidget);
    await _tapLabel(tester, '위생');
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.durationText(190)), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('늦게 온 옛 아이의 "지난번처럼"은 버린다', (tester) async {
    final backend = VisitTestBackend();
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/customers/7'] = (o, b) => FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 200,
      data: customerDetailJson(
        pets: [
          petJson(petId: 11, name: '두부'),
          petJson(petId: 12, name: '보리'),
        ],
      ),
    );
    final slow = Completer<FakeResponse>();
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits/defaults'] = (o, b) {
      if (o.queryParameters['pet_id'] == 11) return slow.future;
      return FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: defaultsJson(
          durationMinutes: 60,
          menus: [menuBriefJson(menuId: 2, name: '위생')],
        ),
      );
    };
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    await _pickCustomer(tester);

    await _tapLabel(tester, '두부');
    await _tapLabel(tester, '두부');
    await _tapLabel(tester, '보리');
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.durationText(60)), findsOneWidget);
    slow.complete(
      FakeResponse(statusCode: 200, data: defaultsJson(durationMinutes: 150)),
    );
    await settle(tester);

    expect(find.text(AppStrings.durationText(60)), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('저장을 두 번 눌러 VISIT_DUPLICATE 면 이미 저장된 것으로 보고 닫는다', (tester) async {
    final backend = VisitTestBackend();
    backend.routes['POST /v1/shops/3/visits'] = (o, b) => const FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 409,
      data: {'code': 'VISIT_DUPLICATE', 'message': 'x'},
    );
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    final result = await _open(tester, setup);
    await _pickCustomer(tester);
    await _tapLabel(tester, '14:00');

    await tester.tap(_save());
    await settle(tester);

    expect(result.closed, isTrue);
    expect(result.value, DateTime(2026, 10, 2));
  });

  testWidgets('서버가 거절하면 문구를 보이고 폼에 남는다', (tester) async {
    final backend = VisitTestBackend();
    backend.routes['POST /v1/shops/3/visits'] = (o, b) => const FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 422,
      data: {'code': 'VISIT_CROSSES_MIDNIGHT', 'message': 'x'},
    );
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    await _pickCustomer(tester);
    await _tapLabel(tester, '14:00');

    await tester.tap(_save());
    await settle(tester);

    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.registerVisit), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.textContaining('자정을 넘습니다'), findsOneWidget);

  testWidgets('저장 중에는 버튼을 막아 요청을 한 번만 보낸다', (tester) async {
    final backend = VisitTestBackend();
    final gate = Completer<FakeResponse>();
    backend.routes['POST /v1/shops/3/visits'] = (o, b) => gate.future;
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    await _pickCustomer(tester);
    await _tapLabel(tester, '14:00');

    await tester.tap(_save());
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(_save(), warnIfMissed: false);
    await tester.pump();
    gate.complete(
      FakeResponse(statusCode: 201, data: savedJson([visitJson()])),
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();

    expect(
      setup.calls.where((c) => c == 'POST /v1/shops/3/visits'),
      hasLength(1),
    );
  });

  testWidgets('[지금 시작]은 시각 없이 walk_in 으로 보낸다', (tester) async {
    final backend = VisitTestBackend();

  testWidgets('다른 날을 보고 있어도 [지금 시작]이 보인다 — 예약 없이 온 손님은 지금 시작한다', (

  testWidgets('새 고객 등록으로 가서 등록하면 그 고객의 ②로 바로 온다', (tester) async {
    final backend = VisitTestBackend();
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/customers/8'] = (o, b) => FakeResponse(

          customerId: 8,

    expect(find.text('한지우 · ··1122'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('다음 예약 초안으로 열면 ①을 건너뛰고 채워 열며, "지난번처럼"을 부르지 않는다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(VisitTestBackend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(
      tester,
      setup,
      path: '/visits/new',
      extra: NextVisitDraft.fromJson(nextDraftJson()),
    );

    expect(find.text(AppStrings.reserveWhenTitle), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.changeCustomer), findsNothing);
    expect(
      find.text(AppStrings.saveReservation('10월 30일 금 14:00')),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.durationText(90)), findsOneWidget);
    expect(setup.calls, isNot(contains('GET /v1/shops/3/visits/defaults')));
  });

  testWidgets('[바꾸기]와 뒤로 가기는 ①로 돌아간다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(VisitTestBackend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    await _pickCustomer(tester);

    await tester.tap(find.text(AppStrings.changeCustomer));
    await settle(tester);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.reserveWhoTitle), findsOneWidget);

    await _pickCustomer(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.back));
    await settle(tester);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.reserveWhoTitle), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.registerVisit), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('예약 ② 골든', (tester) async {
    final backend = VisitTestBackend();
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/customers/7'] = (o, b) => FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 200,
      data: customerDetailJson(
        customer: customerJson(name: '이수진'),
        pets: [
          petJson(petId: 11, name: '두부'),
          petJson(petId: 12, name: '보리'),
        ],
      ),
    );
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits/slots'] = (o, b) {
      final at = o.queryParameters['at'] as String?;
      return FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: at == null
            ? slotsJson(
                warnings: {
                  15: ['overlap'],
                  16: ['overlap'],
                },
              )
            : slotAtJson(at: at),
      );
    };
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    await _pickCustomer(tester);
    await _tapLabel(tester, '두부');
    await _tapLabel(tester, '14:00');
    tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(390, 1100);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();

    await expectLater(
      find.byKey(ScreenKeys.registerVisit),
      matchesGoldenFile('goldens/register_visit_form.png'),
    );
  });
}
```

```dart
import 'package:chagocnote/features/visit/state/visit_form_rules.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

void main() {
  test('메뉴가 없으면 매장 기본 소요다', () {
    expect(menuDuration(const [], 120), 120);
  });

  test('메뉴 기본 소요를 더하고, 기본 소요가 없는 메뉴는 매장 기본 소요로 센다', () {
    expect(menuDuration(const [60, 90], 120), 150);
    expect(menuDuration(const [60, null], 120), 180);
  });

  test('합이 600분을 넘으면 600분이다(넘기면 서버가 422)', () {
    expect(menuDuration(const [300, 300, 60], 120), 600);
  });

  test('한 칸에 10분씩(길게 누르면 30분) 늘리고 줄이되 10~600분을 벗어나지 않는다', () {
    expect(stepDuration(90, longer: true), 100);
    expect(stepDuration(90, longer: false), 80);
    expect(stepDuration(90, longer: true, long: true), 120);
    expect(stepDuration(90, longer: false, long: true), 60);
    expect(stepDuration(10, longer: false), 10);
    expect(stepDuration(590, longer: true, long: true), 600);
  });
}
```

`app/test/support/visit_test_backend.dart`:

```dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:chagocnote/core/router/app_router.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import 'customer_fixtures.dart';
import 'customer_test_app.dart';
import 'fake_http_client_adapter.dart';
import 'visit_fixtures.dart';

/// 예약 화면 테스트의 가짜 서버. 기본 응답을 두고, 테스트가 `METHOD path`
/// 단위로 바꿔 끼운다. 모르는 요청은 실패시킨다.
class VisitTestBackend {
  VisitTestBackend([Map<String, FakeHandler> overrides = const {}]) {
    routes.addAll(overrides);
  }

  final Map<String, FakeHandler> routes = {
    'GET /v1/shops/3/visit-settings': (o, b) =>
        FakeResponse(statusCode: 200, data: settingsJson()),
    'GET /v1/shops/3/menus': (o, b) =>
        FakeResponse(statusCode: 200, data: {'items': defaultMenusJson()}),
    'GET /v1/shops/3/customers': (o, b) =>
        FakeResponse(statusCode: 200, data: pageJson(items: [_family()])),
    'GET /v1/shops/3/customers/7': (o, b) => FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 200,
      data: customerDetailJson(
        customer: customerJson(name: '이수진'),
        pets: [petJson(petId: 11, name: '두부')],
      ),
    ),
    'GET /v1/shops/3/visits/calendar': (o, b) {
      final month = (o.queryParameters['month'] as String).split('-');
      return FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: calendarJson(
          year: int.parse(month[0]),
          month: int.parse(month[1]),
        ),
      );
    },
    'GET /v1/shops/3/visits/slots': (o, b) {
      final date = o.queryParameters['date'] as String;
      final at = o.queryParameters['at'] as String?;
      final duration = o.queryParameters['duration'] as int;
      return FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: at == null
            ? slotsJson(date: date, durationMinutes: duration)
            : slotAtJson(date: date, at: at, durationMinutes: duration),
      );
    },
    'GET /v1/shops/3/visits/defaults': (o, b) =>
        FakeResponse(statusCode: 200, data: defaultsJson()),
    'POST /v1/shops/3/visits': (o, b) =>
        FakeResponse(statusCode: 201, data: savedJson([visitJson()])),
  };

  static Map<String, dynamic> _family() => familyJson(
    customerId: 7,
    name: '이수진',
    pets: [petSummaryJson(petId: 11, name: '두부')],
  );

  FutureOr<FakeResponse> call(RequestOptions options, Uint8List? body) {
    final handler = routes['${options.method} ${options.path}'];
    if (handler == null) {
      fail('예상하지 못한 요청: ${options.method} ${options.path}');
    }
    return handler(options, body);
  }
}

Map<String, dynamic> bodyOf(Uint8List? body) =>
    jsonDecode(utf8.decode(body!)) as Map<String, dynamic>;

/// 요청이 가짜 시간 0 에 걸려 있으면 `pumpAndSettle`만으로는 끝나지 않는다
/// (그릴 프레임이 없으면 시간이 흐르지 않는다) — 1ms 를 흘린 뒤 기다린다.
Future<void> settle(WidgetTester tester) async {
  await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 1));
  await tester.pumpAndSettle();
}

/// 폼·목록 전체가 한 화면에 그려지게 세로로 긴 화면을 쓴다(목록이 게으르게 그린다).
void useTallView(WidgetTester tester, {double height = 2000}) {
  tester.view.physicalSize = Size(390, height);
  tester.view.devicePixelRatio = 1.0;
  addTearDown(tester.view.resetPhysicalSize);
  addTearDown(tester.view.resetDevicePixelRatio);
}

/// 쌓아 연 화면이 닫힐 때 돌려받은 값. 닫히지 않았으면 [closed]가 false 다 —
/// 테스트가 닫힘을 `await`로 기다리면 닫히지 않을 때 멈춰 버린다.
class Closed<T> {
  bool closed = false;
  T? value;
}

/// 홈을 띄우고 [path]를 쌓아 연다.
Future<Closed<T>> pushOnHome<T>(
  WidgetTester tester,
  CustomerTestSetup setup,
  String path, {
  Object? extra,
}) async {
  await tester.pumpWidget(customerRouterApp(setup.container));
  await tester.pumpAndSettle();
  final closed = Closed<T>();
  setup.container
      .read(routerProvider)
      .push<T>(path, extra: extra)
      .then(
        (value) => closed
          ..closed = true
          ..value = value,
      );
  await settle(tester);
  return closed;
}
```

`app/lib/features/visit/screens/register_visit_screen.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../../core/error/api_exception.dart';
import '../../../core/error/error_messages.dart';
import '../../../core/router/screen_keys.dart';
import '../../../design/app_theme.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_radius.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_spacing.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_typography.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_back_button.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_button.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_card.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/pressable.dart';
import '../../../l10n/app_strings.dart';
import '../../auth/state/session.dart';
import '../../auth/state/session_controller.dart';
import '../../customer/api/customer_models.dart';
import '../../customer/state/customer_input.dart';
import '../../customer/state/customer_list_controller.dart';
import '../../customer/state/customer_providers.dart';
import '../../customer/state/search_debouncer.dart';
import '../../home/state/today_provider.dart';
import '../api/visit_models.dart';
import '../state/visit_commands.dart';
import 'visit_form.dart';
import 'visit_labels.dart';

/// 예약 등록 — ① 고객 찾기(고객 탭의 검색 그대로) → ② 날짜·시간·반려동물·
/// 메뉴(설계 9.1). 달력에서 날짜를 고른 뒤 열면 ②의 날짜가 그 날이다.
/// [draft](완료하고 다음 예약 잡기)로 열면 ①을 건너뛰고 ②를 채워 연다.
///
/// 저장하면 저장한 날짜를 돌려주며 닫는다 — 부른 화면이 그 날로 옮겨 간다.
class RegisterVisitScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const RegisterVisitScreen({super.key, this.date, this.draft});

  final DateTime? date;
  final NextVisitDraft? draft;

  @override
  ConsumerState<RegisterVisitScreen> createState() =>
      _RegisterVisitScreenState();
}

class _RegisterVisitScreenState extends ConsumerState<RegisterVisitScreen> {
  late int? _customerId = widget.draft?.customerId;
  bool _busy = false;
  String? _errorText;

  int? get _shopId {
    final session = ref.read(sessionControllerProvider).value;
    return session is SignedIn ? session.selectedShopId : null;
  }

  void _backToSearch() => setState(() {
    _customerId = null;
    _errorText = null;
  });

  Future<void> _save(VisitFormValue value, {bool walkIn = false}) async {
    final shopId = _shopId;
    final customerId = _customerId;
    if (shopId == null || customerId == null || _busy) return;
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _errorText = null;
    });
    try {
      await ref
          .read(visitCommandsProvider)
          .register(
            shopId,
            VisitRequest(
              customerId: customerId,
              petIds: value.petIds,
              startsAt: value.startsAt,
              durationMinutes: value.durationMinutes,
              menuIds: value.menuIds,
              memo: value.memo,
              walkIn: walkIn,
            ),
          );
      if (mounted) context.pop(value.date);
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      // 저장을 두 번 누른 경우다 — 이미 저장됐으니 닫는다(결정 기록 006).
      if (e.code == 'VISIT_DUPLICATE') {
        if (mounted) context.pop(value.date);
        return;
      }
      _fail(e);
    } catch (e) {
      _fail(e);
    }
  }

  void _fail(Object error) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _busy = false;
      _errorText = messageFor(error);
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final shopId = _shopId;
    final customerId = _customerId;
    final onSearch = customerId == null;
    // 초안으로 열었으면 ①이 없다 — 뒤로 가면 닫는다.
    final backToSearch = !onSearch && widget.draft == null;
    return PopScope(
      canPop: !backToSearch,
      onPopInvokedWithResult: (didPop, _) {
        if (!didPop) _backToSearch();
      },
      child: Scaffold(
        key: ScreenKeys.registerVisit,
        body: AppBackground(
          child: SafeArea(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                    AppSpacing.lg,
                    AppSpacing.sm,
                    AppSpacing.lg,
                    AppSpacing.sm,
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      AppBackButton(onTap: backToSearch ? _backToSearch : null),
                      const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),
                      Text(
                        onSearch
                            ? AppStrings.reserveWhoTitle
                            : AppStrings.reserveWhenTitle,
                        style: AppTypography.titleKo,
                      ),
                      const Spacer(),
                      Text(
                        AppStrings.stepOf(onSearch ? 1 : 2, 2),
                        style: AppTypography.caption,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                Expanded(
                  child: shopId == null
                      ? const SizedBox.shrink()
                      : onSearch
                      ? _CustomerSearch(
                          shopId: shopId,
                          onPick: (id) => setState(() => _customerId = id),
                        )
                      : _formFor(shopId, customerId),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _formFor(int shopId, int customerId) {
    final draft = widget.draft;
    final initial = draft != null
        ? VisitFormInitial(
            date: draft.startsAt.day,
            petIds: {...draft.petIds},
            ownerOnly: draft.petIds.isEmpty,
            at: hhmm(draft.startsAt.wall),
            durationMinutes: draft.durationMinutes,
            menus: draft.menus,
            prefilled: true,
          )
        : VisitFormInitial(date: widget.date ?? ref.read(todayProvider));
    return VisitForm(
      // 보호자를 바꾸면 폼을 새로 만든다 — 앞 보호자의 아이 선택이 남지 않게.
      key: ValueKey(customerId),
      shopId: shopId,
      customerId: customerId,
      initial: initial,
      header: _OwnerCard(
        shopId: shopId,
        customerId: customerId,
        onChange: draft == null ? _backToSearch : null,
      ),
      submitLabel: (date, at) =>
          AppStrings.saveReservation('${dayLabel(date)} $at'),
      onSubmit: _save,
      onWalkIn: (value) => _save(value, walkIn: true),
      busy: _busy,
      errorText: _errorText,
    );
  }
}

/// ②의 보호자 카드 — 이름·번호 뒷자리와 [바꾸기].
class _OwnerCard extends ConsumerWidget {
  const _OwnerCard({
    required this.shopId,
    required this.customerId,
    required this.onChange,
  });

  final int shopId;
  final int customerId;
  final VoidCallback? onChange;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final customer = ref
        .watch(customerDetailProvider((shopId: shopId, customerId: customerId)))
        .value
        ?.customer;
    return AppCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        horizontal: AppSpacing.md,
        vertical: AppSpacing.sm,
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              customer == null
                  ? ''
                  : '${customer.name} · ${maskedTail(customer.phone)}',
              style: AppTypography.bodyStrong,
            ),
          ),
          if (onChange != null)
            AppButton(label: AppStrings.changeCustomer, onPressed: onChange),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// ① 고객 찾기 — 고객 탭과 같은 검색(뒷자리·이름·반려동물, 초성)과 목록.
class _CustomerSearch extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const _CustomerSearch({required this.shopId, required this.onPick});

  final int shopId;
  final ValueChanged<int> onPick;

  @override
  ConsumerState<_CustomerSearch> createState() => _CustomerSearchState();
}

class _CustomerSearchState extends ConsumerState<_CustomerSearch> {
  final _search = TextEditingController();
  late final _debouncer = SearchDebouncer(
    onSearch: (q) => setState(() => _q = q),
  );
  String _q = '';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _search.addListener(() => _debouncer.onChanged(_search.value));
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _debouncer.dispose();
    _search.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  /// 새 고객을 등록하고 돌아오면 그 고객으로 ②에 바로 간다.
  Future<void> _registerNew(BuildContext context) async {
    final customerId = await context.push<int>('/customers/new?pick=1');
    if (customerId != null && mounted) widget.onPick(customerId);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final query = (
      shopId: widget.shopId,
      q: _q,
      sort: 'recent',
      includePassed: false,
    );
    final list = ref.watch(customerListControllerProvider(query));
    return Column(
      children: [
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: AppSpacing.lg),
          child: TextField(
            controller: _search,
            autofocus: true,
            textInputAction: TextInputAction.search,
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              hintText: AppStrings.customerSearchHint,
              hintStyle: AppTypography.body.copyWith(
                color: AppColors.secondary,
              ),
              prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search, color: AppColors.secondary),
              filled: true,
              fillColor: AppColors.white,
              isDense: true,
              border: OutlineInputBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadius.md),
                borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.line),
              ),
              enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadius.md),
                borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.line),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),
        Expanded(
          child: list.when(
            loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
            error: (error, _) => Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Text(messageFor(error), style: AppTypography.body),
                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
                  AppButton(
                    label: AppStrings.retry,
                    onPressed: () =>
                        ref.invalidate(customerListControllerProvider(query)),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            data: (state) => ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                AppSpacing.lg,
                0,
                AppSpacing.lg,
                AppSpacing.xxl,
              ),
              children: [
                for (final card in state.items)
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.sm),
                    child: _FamilyRow(
                      card: card,
                      onTap: () => widget.onPick(card.customerId),
                    ),
                  ),
                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),
                Center(
                  child: Text(
                    AppStrings.reserveNoCustomer,
                    style: AppTypography.caption,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),
                Center(
                  child: AppButton(
                    label: AppStrings.registerNewCustomer,
                    onPressed: () => _registerNew(context),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _FamilyRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const _FamilyRow({required this.card, required this.onTap});

  final FamilyCard card;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final names = card.pets.map((p) => p.name).join(' · ');
    return Pressable(
      semanticLabel: '${card.name} $names',
      onTap: onTap,
      child: AppCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          horizontal: AppSpacing.md,
          vertical: AppSpacing.sm,
        ),
        child: SizedBox(
          width: double.infinity,
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(names, style: AppTypography.bodyStrong),
              Text(
                '${card.name} · ${card.phoneMasked}',
                style: AppTypography.caption,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

`app/lib/features/visit/screens/visit_form.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../../core/error/error_messages.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_radius.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_spacing.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_typography.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_button.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_card.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_choice_chip.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_sheet.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/pressable.dart';
import '../../../l10n/app_strings.dart';
import '../../customer/api/customer_models.dart';
import '../../customer/state/customer_providers.dart';
import '../api/visit_models.dart';
import '../state/visit_commands.dart';
import '../state/visit_form_rules.dart';
import '../state/visit_providers.dart';
import 'visit_labels.dart';

/// 예약 폼이 내보내는 값. [petIds]가 비었으면 보호자만의 방문이다.
class VisitFormValue {
  const VisitFormValue({
    required this.petIds,
    required this.date,
    required this.startsAt,
    required this.durationMinutes,
    required this.menuIds,
    required this.memo,
  });

  final List<int> petIds;
  final DateTime date;

  /// 서버가 시간 칸으로 준 시각 문자열. 지금 시작이면 null.
  final String? startsAt;
  final int durationMinutes;
  final List<int> menuIds;
  final String? memo;
}

/// 폼의 처음 값. [prefilled]면 반려동물을 골라도 "지난번처럼"으로 덮어쓰지
/// 않는다(다음 예약 초안·수정).
class VisitFormInitial {
  const VisitFormInitial({
    required this.date,
    this.petIds = const {},
    this.ownerOnly = false,
    this.at,
    this.durationMinutes,
    this.menus = const [],
    this.memo,
    this.prefilled = false,
  });

  final DateTime date;
  final Set<int> petIds;
  final bool ownerOnly;

  /// `HH:MM`.
  final String? at;
  final int? durationMinutes;
  final List<MenuBrief> menus;
  final String? memo;
  final bool prefilled;
}

/// 예약 ②(등록)와 수정이 함께 쓰는 폼 — 반려동물 칩, 날짜 줄, 시간 칸,
/// 메뉴·소요, 메모, 저장 버튼(설계 9.1).
///
/// 시간 칸의 줄긋기와 경고 문구는 서버의 시간 칸 응답만 쓴다(설계 6.3).
/// 고른 시각도 서버에 한 번 더 물어 그 응답의 시각 문자열을 저장에 쓴다 —
/// 매장 시간대를 앱이 계산하지 않는다.
class VisitForm extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const VisitForm({
    super.key,
    required this.shopId,
    required this.customerId,
    required this.initial,
    required this.header,
    required this.submitLabel,
    required this.onSubmit,
    required this.busy,
    required this.errorText,
    this.onWalkIn,
    this.singlePet = false,
    this.scheduleLocked = false,
    this.excludeVisitIds = const [],
  });

  final int shopId;
  final int customerId;
  final VisitFormInitial initial;

  /// 폼 맨 위(보호자 카드).
  final Widget header;
  final String Function(DateTime date, String at) submitLabel;
  final Future<void> Function(VisitFormValue value) onSubmit;

  /// 있으면 오늘을 고른 동안 [지금 시작]을 보인다(예약 없이 온 손님).
  final Future<void> Function(VisitFormValue value)? onWalkIn;
  final bool busy;
  final String? errorText;

  /// 수정 — 방문 하나는 반려동물 하나(또는 보호자만)다.
  final bool singlePet;

  /// 수정 — 예약됨이 아니면 날짜·시간·반려동물을 바꾸지 않는다(설계 6.2).
  final bool scheduleLocked;

  /// 옮길 방문. 시간 칸이 자기 자신과 겹치지 않게 뺀다.
  final List<int> excludeVisitIds;

  @override
  ConsumerState<VisitForm> createState() => _VisitFormState();
}

class _VisitFormState extends ConsumerState<VisitForm> {
  late final _memo = TextEditingController(text: widget.initial.memo);

  late Set<int> _petIds = {...widget.initial.petIds};
  late bool _ownerOnly = widget.initial.ownerOnly;
  late DateTime _date = widget.initial.date;
  late DateTime _stripStart = _weekStart(widget.initial.date);
  late String? _at = widget.initial.at;
  late int? _duration = widget.initial.durationMinutes;
  late List<int> _menuIds = [for (final m in widget.initial.menus) m.menuId];

  /// 보관돼 메뉴표에 없지만 이 방문에 붙어 있는 메뉴(이름을 보이려고 둔다).
  late List<MenuBrief> _knownMenus = [...widget.initial.menus];

  bool _menusTouched = false;
  bool _durationTouched = false;
  bool _prefilledNote = false;
  bool _petsInitialized = false;

  /// "지난번처럼"은 반려동물을 고를 때마다 나갈 수 있다 — 늦게 온 옛 결과를 버린다.
  int _defaultsSeq = 0;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _memo.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  static DateTime _weekStart(DateTime d) =>
      DateTime(d.year, d.month, d.day - (d.weekday - 1));

  String get _exclude => widget.excludeVisitIds.join(',');

  int _effectiveDuration(VisitSettings settings) =>
      _duration ?? settings.defaultDurationMinutes;

  /// 반려동물이 하나뿐인 보호자면 그 아이를 미리 고른다.
  void _initPets(List<Pet> living) {
    if (_petsInitialized) return;
    _petsInitialized = true;
    if (_petIds.isNotEmpty || _ownerOnly || living.length != 1) return;
    _petIds = {living.single.petId};
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback(
      (_) => _prefill(living.single.petId),
    );
  }

  void _togglePet(int petId) {
    setState(() {
      _ownerOnly = false;
      if (widget.singlePet) {
        _petIds = {petId};
      } else if (!_petIds.remove(petId)) {
        _petIds.add(petId);
      }
    });
    // 한 마리만 남으면(처음 골랐든, 하나를 풀어 하나가 남았든) 그 아이로 채운다.
    if (_petIds.length == 1) _prefill(_petIds.single);
  }

  void _chooseOwnerOnly() {
    setState(() {
      _ownerOnly = true;
      _petIds = {};
    });
  }

  /// 처음 고른 아이의 지난 완료 방문 메뉴·소요로 채운다. 이미 손댔거나
  /// 초안·수정으로 채워진 폼이면 두고, 실패해도 그대로 둔다(돕는 기능이다).
  Future<void> _prefill(int petId) async {
    if (widget.initial.prefilled || _menusTouched || _durationTouched) return;
    final seq = ++_defaultsSeq;
    try {
      final defaults = await ref
          .read(visitCommandsProvider)
          .defaults(widget.shopId, petId);
      if (!mounted || seq != _defaultsSeq) return;
      if (_menusTouched || _durationTouched) return;
      setState(() {
        _menuIds = [for (final m in defaults.menus) m.menuId];
        _knownMenus = [..._knownMenus, ...defaults.menus];
        _duration = defaults.durationMinutes;
        _prefilledNote = defaults.sourceVisitId != null;
      });
    } catch (_) {
      // 미리 채우기는 돕는 것뿐이다 — 실패하면 빈 채로 둔다.
    }
  }

  void _toggleMenu(Menu menu, List<Menu> all, VisitSettings settings) {
    setState(() {
      _menusTouched = true;
      _prefilledNote = false;
      if (!_menuIds.remove(menu.menuId)) _menuIds.add(menu.menuId);
      if (!_durationTouched) {
        final byId = {for (final m in all) m.menuId: m};
        _duration = menuDuration([
          for (final id in _menuIds)
            byId[id]?.defaultDurationMinutes ?? settings.defaultDurationMinutes,
        ], settings.defaultDurationMinutes);
      }
    });
  }

  void _stepDuration(
    VisitSettings settings, {
    required bool longer,
    bool long = false,
  }) {
    setState(() {
      _durationTouched = true;
      _prefilledNote = false;
      _duration = stepDuration(
        _effectiveDuration(settings),
        longer: longer,
        long: long,
      );
    });
  }

  void _pickDate(DateTime date) => setState(() => _date = date);

  Future<void> _pickMinute(DateTime hour) async {
    final picked = await AppSheet.show<String>(
      context,
      builder: (context) => Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          const Text(AppStrings.pickMinuteTitle, style: AppTypography.body),
          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),
          Wrap(
            spacing: AppSpacing.sm,
            runSpacing: AppSpacing.sm,
            children: [
              for (var m = 0; m < 60; m += 10)
                AppChoiceChip(
                  label: hhmm(DateTime(2000, 1, 1, hour.hour, m)),
                  selected: false,
                  onTap: () => Navigator.of(
                    context,
                  ).pop(hhmm(DateTime(2000, 1, 1, hour.hour, m))),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (picked != null && mounted) setState(() => _at = picked);
  }

  VisitFormValue _value(VisitSettings settings, String? startsAt) {
    final memo = _memo.text.trim();
    return VisitFormValue(
      petIds: _ownerOnly ? const [] : _petIds.toList(),
      date: _date,
      startsAt: startsAt,
      durationMinutes: _effectiveDuration(settings),
      menuIds: [..._menuIds],
      memo: memo.isEmpty ? null : memo,
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final settings = ref.watch(visitSettingsProvider(widget.shopId));
    final customer = ref.watch(
      customerDetailProvider((
        shopId: widget.shopId,
        customerId: widget.customerId,
      )),
    );
    final menus = ref.watch(
      menusProvider((shopId: widget.shopId, includeArchived: false)),
    );
    final error = settings.error ?? customer.error ?? menus.error;
    if (error != null) {
      return Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Text(messageFor(error), style: AppTypography.body),
            const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
            AppButton(
              label: AppStrings.retry,
              onPressed: () {
                ref.invalidate(visitSettingsProvider(widget.shopId));
                ref.invalidate(customerDetailProvider);
                ref.invalidate(menusProvider);
              },
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }
    final s = settings.value;
    final c = customer.value;
    final m = menus.value;
    if (s == null || c == null || m == null) {
      return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
    }
    final living = [
      for (final p in c.pets)
        if (!p.passedAway || widget.initial.petIds.contains(p.petId)) p,
    ];
    _initPets(living);
    return _buildForm(s, living, m);
  }

  Widget _buildForm(VisitSettings settings, List<Pet> pets, List<Menu> menus) {
    final duration = _effectiveDuration(settings);
    final selection = _at == null
        ? null
        : ref.watch(
            visitSlotsProvider((
              shopId: widget.shopId,
              date: _date,
              duration: duration,
              at: _at,
              exclude: _exclude,
            )),
          );
    final chosen = selection?.value?.slots.firstOrNull;
    final petsChosen = _ownerOnly || _petIds.isNotEmpty;
    final canSubmit =
        petsChosen && chosen != null && !widget.busy && _at != null;

    return ListView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
        AppSpacing.lg,
        AppSpacing.sm,
        AppSpacing.lg,
        AppSpacing.xxl,
      ),
      children: [
        widget.header,
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),
        _PetChips(
          pets: pets,
          selected: _petIds,
          ownerOnly: _ownerOnly,
          locked: widget.scheduleLocked,
          onPet: _togglePet,
          onOwnerOnly: _chooseOwnerOnly,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
        _DateStrip(
          shopId: widget.shopId,
          start: _stripStart,
          selected: _date,
          locked: widget.scheduleLocked,
          onMove: (days) => setState(
            () => _stripStart = DateTime(
              _stripStart.year,
              _stripStart.month,
              _stripStart.day + days,
            ),
          ),
          onPick: _pickDate,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
        Row(
          children: [
            const Text(AppStrings.visitTimeLabel, style: AppTypography.caption),
            const Spacer(),
            Text(
              AppStrings.businessHours(settings.opensAt, settings.closesAt),
              style: AppTypography.caption,
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),
        _SlotGrid(
          slotsKey: (
            shopId: widget.shopId,
            date: _date,
            duration: duration,
            at: null,
            exclude: _exclude,
          ),
          selectedAt: _at,
          locked: widget.scheduleLocked,
          onPick: (at) => setState(() => _at = at),
          onLongPress: _pickMinute,
        ),
        if (!widget.scheduleLocked)
          const Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: AppSpacing.xs),
            child: Text(
              AppStrings.pickMinuteHint,
              style: AppTypography.caption,
            ),
          ),
        if (chosen != null)
          for (final w in chosen.warnings)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: AppSpacing.xs),
              child: Text(
                '· ${warningLabel(w)}',
                style: AppTypography.bodyStrong,
              ),
            ),
        if (selection?.error != null)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: AppSpacing.xs),
            child: Text(
              messageFor(selection!.error!),
              style: AppTypography.caption,
            ),
          ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
        _MenuSection(
          menus: menus,
          knownMenus: _knownMenus,
          selectedIds: _menuIds,
          duration: duration,
          prefilledNote: _prefilledNote,
          onToggle: (menu) => _toggleMenu(menu, menus, settings),
          onShorter: ({bool long = false}) =>
              _stepDuration(settings, longer: false, long: long),
          onLonger: ({bool long = false}) =>
              _stepDuration(settings, longer: true, long: long),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
        AppCard(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: AppSpacing.md),
          child: TextField(
            controller: _memo,
            maxLines: 3,
            minLines: 1,
            inputFormatters: [LengthLimitingTextInputFormatter(500)],
            decoration: const InputDecoration(
              hintText: AppStrings.visitMemoHint,
              border: InputBorder.none,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
        if (widget.errorText != null)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.sm),
            child: Text(widget.errorText!, style: AppTypography.caption),
          ),
        AppButton(
          label: !petsChosen
              ? AppStrings.pickPetFirst
              : _at == null
              ? AppStrings.pickTimeFirst
              : widget.submitLabel(_date, _at!),
          filled: true,
          busy: widget.busy,
          onPressed: canSubmit
              ? () => widget.onSubmit(_value(settings, chosen.startsAt.iso))
              : null,
        ),
        // 예약 없이 온 손님은 어느 날을 보고 있든 지금 시작한다 — 시각은 서버가 지금으로 놓는다.
        if (widget.onWalkIn != null) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),
          AppButton(
            label: AppStrings.startNow,
            onPressed: petsChosen && !widget.busy
                ? () => widget.onWalkIn!(_value(settings, null))
                : null,
          ),
        ],
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _PetChips extends StatelessWidget {
  const _PetChips({
    required this.pets,
    required this.selected,
    required this.ownerOnly,
    required this.locked,
    required this.onPet,
    required this.onOwnerOnly,
  });

  final List<Pet> pets;
  final Set<int> selected;
  final bool ownerOnly;
  final bool locked;
  final ValueChanged<int> onPet;
  final VoidCallback onOwnerOnly;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Wrap(
      spacing: AppSpacing.sm,
      runSpacing: AppSpacing.sm,
      children: [
        for (final p in pets)
          AppChoiceChip(
            label: p.name,
            selected: selected.contains(p.petId),
            onTap: locked ? null : () => onPet(p.petId),
          ),
        AppChoiceChip(
          label: AppStrings.ownerOnly,
          selected: ownerOnly,
          onTap: locked ? null : onOwnerOnly,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// 날짜 줄 — 한 주(월~일)를 보이고 ‹ › 로 한 주씩 옮긴다. 휴무일은 옅게
/// 보이지만 고를 수 있다(경고만, 설계 2절).
class _DateStrip extends ConsumerWidget {
  const _DateStrip({
    required this.shopId,
    required this.start,
    required this.selected,
    required this.locked,
    required this.onMove,
    required this.onPick,
  });

  final int shopId;
  final DateTime start;
  final DateTime selected;
  final bool locked;
  final ValueChanged<int> onMove;
  final ValueChanged<DateTime> onPick;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final days = [
      for (var i = 0; i < 7; i++)
        DateTime(start.year, start.month, start.day + i),
    ];
    // 한 주가 두 달에 걸치면 두 달을 다 읽는다.
    final months = {for (final d in days) DateTime(d.year, d.month)};
    final closed = <DateTime>{};
    for (final month in months) {
      final summary = ref
          .watch(calendarMonthProvider((shopId: shopId, month: month)))
          .value;
      for (final d in summary?.days ?? const <CalendarDay>[]) {
        if (d.closedDay) closed.add(d.date);
      }
    }
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Row(
          children: [
            const Text(AppStrings.visitDateLabel, style: AppTypography.caption),
            const Spacer(),
            Text(
              AppStrings.monthLabel(selected.month),
              style: AppTypography.bodyStrong,
            ),
            Pressable(
              semanticLabel: AppStrings.prevWeek,
              onTap: locked ? null : () => onMove(-7),
              child: const Icon(Icons.chevron_left, size: 20),
            ),
            Pressable(
              semanticLabel: AppStrings.nextWeek,
              onTap: locked ? null : () => onMove(7),
              child: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size: 20),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        Row(
          children: [
            for (final d in days)
              Expanded(
                child: _DayCell(
                  date: d,
                  selected: sameDay(d, selected),
                  closed: closed.contains(d),
                  onTap: locked ? null : () => onPick(d),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _DayCell extends StatelessWidget {
  const _DayCell({
    required this.date,
    required this.selected,
    required this.closed,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  final DateTime date;
  final bool selected;
  final bool closed;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final color = selected
        ? AppColors.white
        : closed
        ? AppColors.line
        : AppColors.ink;
    return Pressable(
      semanticLabel: dayLabel(date),
      selected: selected,
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 2),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppSpacing.xs),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: selected ? AppColors.ink : AppColors.white,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadius.md),
          border: Border.all(color: selected ? AppColors.ink : AppColors.line),
        ),
        child: ExcludeSemantics(
          child: Column(
            children: [
              Text(
                AppStrings.weekdaysShort[date.weekday - 1],
                style: AppTypography.caption.copyWith(
                  color: selected ? AppColors.white : AppColors.secondary,
                ),
              ),
              Text(
                '${date.day}',
                style: AppTypography.bodyStrong.copyWith(color: color),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 정시 칸. 겹치는 칸은 줄을 긋고, 휴무·영업시간 밖은 옅게 그린다 — 그래도
/// 누를 수 있다(경고만). 길게 누르면 그 시간의 10분 단위를 고른다.
class _SlotGrid extends ConsumerWidget {
  const _SlotGrid({
    required this.slotsKey,
    required this.selectedAt,
    required this.locked,
    required this.onPick,
    required this.onLongPress,
  });

  final SlotsKey slotsKey;
  final String? selectedAt;
  final bool locked;
  final ValueChanged<String> onPick;
  final ValueChanged<DateTime> onLongPress;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final slots = ref.watch(visitSlotsProvider(slotsKey));
    return slots.when(
      loading: () => const Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.all(AppSpacing.md),
        child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
      ),
      error: (error, _) => Row(
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: Text(messageFor(error), style: AppTypography.caption),
          ),
          AppButton(
            label: AppStrings.retry,
            onPressed: () => ref.invalidate(visitSlotsProvider(slotsKey)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      data: (data) => Wrap(
        spacing: AppSpacing.sm,
        runSpacing: AppSpacing.sm,
        children: [
          for (final slot in data.slots)
            _SlotChip(
              slot: slot,
              selectedAt: selectedAt,
              onTap: locked ? null : () => onPick(hhmm(slot.startsAt.wall)),
              onLongPress: locked
                  ? null
                  : () => onLongPress(slot.startsAt.wall),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SlotChip extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SlotChip({
    required this.slot,
    required this.selectedAt,
    required this.onTap,
    required this.onLongPress,
  });

  final Slot slot;
  final String? selectedAt;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;
  final VoidCallback? onLongPress;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final hour = slot.startsAt.wall.hour;
    // 10분 단위로 고른 시각은 그 시(時)의 칸에 보인다.
    final selectedHere =
        selectedAt != null && int.parse(selectedAt!.substring(0, 2)) == hour;
    final label = selectedHere ? selectedAt! : hhmm(slot.startsAt.wall);
    final overlap = slot.warnings.contains(VisitWarning.overlap);
    final faint =
        slot.warnings.contains(VisitWarning.closedDay) ||
        slot.warnings.contains(VisitWarning.outsideHours);
    return Pressable(
      semanticLabel: label,
      selected: selectedHere,
      onTap: onTap,
      onLongPress: onLongPress,
      child: Container(
        width: 64,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppSpacing.sm),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: selectedHere ? AppColors.ink : AppColors.white,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadius.pill),
          border: Border.all(
            color: selectedHere ? AppColors.ink : AppColors.line,
          ),
        ),
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        child: ExcludeSemantics(
          child: Text(
            label,
            style: AppTypography.body.copyWith(
              color: selectedHere
                  ? AppColors.white
                  : faint || overlap
                  ? AppColors.secondary
                  : AppColors.ink,
              decoration: overlap ? TextDecoration.lineThrough : null,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _MenuSection extends StatelessWidget {
  const _MenuSection({
    required this.menus,
    required this.knownMenus,
    required this.selectedIds,
    required this.duration,
    required this.prefilledNote,
    required this.onToggle,
    required this.onShorter,
    required this.onLonger,
  });

  final List<Menu> menus;
  final List<MenuBrief> knownMenus;
  final List<int> selectedIds;
  final int duration;
  final bool prefilledNote;
  final ValueChanged<Menu> onToggle;
  final void Function({bool long}) onShorter;
  final void Function({bool long}) onLonger;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final activeIds = {for (final m in menus) m.menuId};
    // 보관된 메뉴가 이 방문에 붙어 있으면 이름만 보이고 뺄 수 있게 한다.
    final archived = [
      for (final m in knownMenus)
        if (!activeIds.contains(m.menuId) && selectedIds.contains(m.menuId))
          Menu(
            menuId: m.menuId,
            name: m.name,
            defaultDurationMinutes: null,
            sortOrder: 0,
            archived: true,
          ),
    ];
    return AppCard(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text(
            AppStrings.menuDurationLabel,
            style: AppTypography.caption,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),
          Wrap(
            spacing: AppSpacing.sm,
            runSpacing: AppSpacing.sm,
            children: [
              for (final m in [...menus, ...archived])
                AppChoiceChip(
                  label: m.name,
                  selected: selectedIds.contains(m.menuId),
                  onTap: () => onToggle(m),
                ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),
          Row(
            children: [
              const Text(AppStrings.durationLabel, style: AppTypography.body),
              const Spacer(),
              Pressable(
                semanticLabel: AppStrings.shorter,
                onTap: () => onShorter(),
                onLongPress: () => onShorter(long: true),
                child: const Icon(Icons.remove, size: 18),
              ),
              Text(
                AppStrings.durationText(duration),
                style: AppTypography.bodyStrong,
              ),
              Pressable(
                semanticLabel: AppStrings.longer,
                onTap: () => onLonger(),
                onLongPress: () => onLonger(long: true),
                child: const Icon(Icons.add, size: 18),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          if (prefilledNote) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),
            const Text(
              AppStrings.prefilledFromLast,
              style: AppTypography.caption,
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

`app/lib/features/visit/screens/visit_labels.dart`:

```dart
import '../../../l10n/app_strings.dart';
import '../api/visit_models.dart';

/// `14:00`.
String hhmm(DateTime t) =>
    '${t.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${t.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

/// `10월 2일 금`.
String dayLabel(DateTime d) =>
    '${d.month}월 ${d.day}일 ${AppStrings.weekdaysShort[d.weekday - 1]}';

bool sameDay(DateTime a, DateTime b) =>
    a.year == b.year && a.month == b.month && a.day == b.day;

String statusLabel(String status) => switch (status) {
  VisitStatus.reserved => AppStrings.statusReserved,
  VisitStatus.inProgress => AppStrings.statusInProgress,
  VisitStatus.completed => AppStrings.statusCompleted,
  VisitStatus.canceled => AppStrings.statusCanceled,
  VisitStatus.noShow => AppStrings.statusNoShow,
  _ => status,
};

String warningLabel(String warning) => switch (warning) {
  VisitWarning.overlap => AppStrings.warningOverlap,
  VisitWarning.closedDay => AppStrings.warningClosedDay,
  VisitWarning.outsideHours => AppStrings.warningOutsideHours,
  _ => warning,
};

/// 보호자 이름. 익명화한 고객이면 "삭제된 고객".
String ownerLabel(CustomerBrief customer) =>
    customer.name ?? AppStrings.deletedCustomer;

/// 묶음의 이름 — 반려동물 이름을 잇고, 보호자만의 방문이면 "보호자만".
String visitsTitle(List<Visit> visits) {
  final names = [
    for (final v in visits)
      v.pet == null
          ? AppStrings.ownerOnly
          : (v.pet!.name ?? AppStrings.deletedCustomer),
  ];
  return names.join(' · ');
}

String menusLabel(List<MenuBrief> menus) =>
    menus.isEmpty ? AppStrings.noMenu : menus.map((m) => m.name).join(', ');
```

`app/lib/features/visit/state/visit_form_rules.dart`:

```dart
/// 예약 폼의 소요 규칙. 서버 규칙(10~600분, 10분 단위)을 앱에서 먼저 지킨다 —
/// 최종 판단은 서버다.
library;

const minDuration = 10;
const maxDuration = 600;

/// [+]·[−] 한 번에 바뀌는 소요. 길게 누르면 [durationLongStep].
const durationStep = 10;
const durationLongStep = 30;

int clampDuration(int minutes) =>
    minutes.clamp(minDuration, maxDuration).toInt();

/// 고른 메뉴로 채우는 소요 — 메뉴 기본 소요의 합(기본 소요가 없는 메뉴는
/// 매장 기본 소요), 메뉴가 없으면 매장 기본 소요. 600분을 넘지 않는다.
/// 서버는 소요를 비우면 같은 합을 쓰지만 600을 넘으면 422 라, 앱은 늘
/// 채워 보낸다(결정 기록 006).
int menuDuration(Iterable<int?> menuDefaults, int shopDefault) {
  final list = menuDefaults.toList();
  if (list.isEmpty) return shopDefault;
  final sum = list.fold<int>(0, (total, m) => total + (m ?? shopDefault));
  return clampDuration(sum);
}

/// 소요를 한 칸(10분, [long]이면 30분) 늘리거나 줄인다.
int stepDuration(int minutes, {required bool longer, bool long = false}) {
  final step = long ? durationLongStep : durationStep;
  return clampDuration(minutes + (longer ? step : -step));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 골든을 만든다**

Run (`app/`): `flutter test test/features/visit/screens/register_visit_screen_test.dart --update-goldens --timeout 20s`
Expected: 골든 파일이 생긴다(`register_visit_form.png`). 눈으로 열어 목업과 견준다. **기존 골든이 이 작업의 의도 밖에서 바뀌면 멈추고 보고한다.**

- [ ] **Step 5: 테스트가 통과하는지 확인한다**

Run (`app/`): `flutter test test/features/visit/screens/register_visit_screen_test.dart test/features/visit/state/visit_form_rules_test.dart --timeout 20s`
Expected: 23개 통과(화면 19 — 골든 포함, 규칙 4). 이어서 `flutter test test/features/customer/screens/register_customer_screen_test.dart --timeout 20s` → 11개(pick 모드 1 포함)

Run (`app/`): `flutter analyze` → `No issues found!`, `flutter test --timeout 30s` → 전부 통과(383개).

- [ ] **Step 6: 판별력을 확인한다(변이)**

각 변이를 하나씩 넣고 그 테스트가 실패하는지 본 뒤 되돌린다.

- `_prefill`의 `seq != _defaultsSeq` 비교를 뺀다 → `늦게 온 옛 아이의 "지난번처럼"은 버린다` 실패.
- `_toggleMenu`의 `if (!_durationTouched)`를 `if (true)`로 → `메뉴를 고르면 소요를 메뉴 합으로 채우고, 소요를 손대면…` 실패.
- `_save`의 `VISIT_DUPLICATE` 분기를 뺀다 → `저장을 두 번 눌러 VISIT_DUPLICATE 면…` 실패.
- `_initPets`의 미리 고르기를 뺀다 → 아홉 테스트 실패(한 마리 보호자를 미리 고르지 않는다).
- 무지개다리 거르기(`!p.passedAway || …`)를 뺀다 → `두 마리를 고르면 한 번에 둘을…` 실패(별이 칩이 생긴다).
- `canSubmit`에서 `petsChosen &&`를 뺀다 → `두 마리를 고르면…` 실패(막힌 버튼이 눌린다). `!widget.busy`는 `AppButton`과 겹쳐 판별되지 않는다(알려진 예외).
- `RegisterCustomerScreen._register`의 `pickForReservation` 분기를 뺀다 → `예약 ①에서 열었으면(?pick=1) 등록한 고객 ID 를 돌려주며 닫는다` 실패.
- `_registerNew`에서 돌아온 ID 로 `onPick`을 부르지 않는다 → `새 고객 등록으로 가서 등록하면 그 고객의 ②로 바로 온다` 실패.
- `_togglePet`의 `if (_petIds.length == 1) _prefill(_petIds.single)`을 `contains(petId)` 조건으로 되돌린다 → 지금 테스트로는 판별되지 않는다(두부→보리→두부 해제 경로의 테스트가 없다). **실행자가 하나 더한다**: 두부·보리를 고른 뒤 두부를 풀면 `defaults?pet_id=12`를 묻는다.

### Task 5: 방문 수정

`VisitEditScreen`(`/visits/:visitId/edit`) — 같은 폼을 `singlePet`·`scheduleLocked`(예약됨이 아니면 날짜·시간·반려동물 잠금)·`excludeVisitIds`(묶음 전체)로 연다. 묶음의 시간을 바꾸면 "보리도 같이 옮길까요?"를 묻고, [같이 옮기기]면 예약됨인 형제와 함께 `reschedule` 뒤 PUT(새로 정한 것 9, V6·A4).
