- Modify: `app/lib/core/router/app_router.dart`
- Modify: `app/lib/core/router/screen_keys.dart`
- Create: `app/lib/features/visit/screens/visit_edit_screen.dart`
- Modify: `app/lib/l10n/app_strings.dart`
- Modify: `app/test/design/glass_boundary_test.dart`
- Create: `app/test/features/visit/screens/visit_edit_screen_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4 의 `VisitForm`·`VisitFormInitial`, Task 3 의 `visitDetailProvider`·`VisitCommands.reschedule/update`.
- Produces: `VisitEditScreen(visitId)`, `ScreenKeys.visitEdit`, 라우트 `/visits/:visitId/edit`, 문구 `editVisitTitle/moveTogether/moveOnlyThis/moveTogetherQuestion`.

- [ ] **Step 1: 테스트를 쓴다**

아래 테스트 파일을 그대로 만든다(수정하는 테스트는 diff 대로 고친다).

`app/test/features/visit/screens/visit_edit_screen_test.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:chagocnote/core/router/screen_keys.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/design/widgets/app_button.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/design/widgets/app_choice_chip.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/features/home/state/today_provider.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/l10n/app_strings.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import '../../../support/customer_fixtures.dart';
import '../../../support/customer_test_app.dart';
import '../../../support/fake_http_client_adapter.dart';
import '../../../support/visit_fixtures.dart';
import '../../../support/visit_test_backend.dart';

final _today = [todayProvider.overrideWithValue(DateTime(2026, 9, 23))];

/// 두부(101)·보리(102) 묶음. [status]는 두부의 상태.
VisitTestBackend _backend({String status = 'reserved', bool group = true}) {
  final dubu = visitJson(status: status);
  final bori = visitJson(
    visitId: 102,
    pet: visitPetJson(petId: 12, name: '보리'),
  );
  final backend = VisitTestBackend({
    'GET /v1/shops/3/visits/101': (o, b) => FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 200,
      data: visitDetailJson(visit: dubu, group: group ? [dubu, bori] : [dubu]),
    ),
    'GET /v1/shops/3/customers/7': (o, b) => FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 200,
      data: customerDetailJson(
        customer: customerJson(name: '이수진'),
        pets: [
          petJson(petId: 11, name: '두부'),
          petJson(petId: 12, name: '보리'),
        ],
      ),
    ),
    'PUT /v1/shops/3/visits/101': (o, b) => FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 200,
      data: {'visit': visitJson(), 'warnings': <String>[]},
    ),
    'POST /v1/shops/3/visits/reschedule': (o, b) =>
        FakeResponse(statusCode: 200, data: savedJson([visitJson()])),
  });
  return backend;
}

Future<Closed<Object?>> _open(WidgetTester tester, CustomerTestSetup setup) {
  useTallView(tester);
  return pushOnHome<Object?>(tester, setup, '/visits/101/edit');
}

Future<void> _tapLabel(WidgetTester tester, String label) async {
  await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel(label).first);
  await settle(tester);
}

Finder _saveButton() =>
    find.byWidgetPredicate((w) => w is AppButton && w.label == AppStrings.save);

Future<void> _save(WidgetTester tester) async {
  await tester.tap(_saveButton());
  await settle(tester);
}

void main() {
  testWidgets('예약됨이면 지금 값으로 채워 열고, 고친 값을 PUT 으로 모두 보낸 뒤 닫는다', (tester) async {
    final backend = _backend(group: false);
    Map<String, dynamic>? sent;
    backend.routes['PUT /v1/shops/3/visits/101'] = (o, body) {
      sent = bodyOf(body);
      return FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: {'visit': visitJson(), 'warnings': <String>[]},
      );
    };
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    final closed = await _open(tester, setup);

    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.visitEdit), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('이수진 · ${AppStrings.statusReserved}'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.durationText(90)), findsOneWidget);
    await _tapLabel(tester, '15:00');
    await _tapLabel(tester, '보리');
    await tester.enterText(find.byType(TextField), '발톱 길게');
    await _save(tester);

    expect(sent, {
      'starts_at': '2026-10-02T15:00:00+09:00',
      'pet_id': 12,
      'duration_minutes': 90,
      'menu_ids': [1],
      'memo': '발톱 길게',
    });
    expect(setup.calls, isNot(contains('GET /v1/shops/3/visits/defaults')));
    expect(closed.closed, isTrue);
  });

  testWidgets('방문 하나는 한 아이다 — 다른 아이를 고르면 바꿔 끼운다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(
      _backend(group: false).call,
      overrides: _today,
    );
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await _tapLabel(tester, '보리');

    bool selected(String label) => tester
        .widget<AppChoiceChip>(
          find.byWidgetPredicate((w) => w is AppChoiceChip && w.label == label),
        )
        .selected;
    expect(selected('보리'), isTrue);
    expect(selected('두부'), isFalse);
  });

  testWidgets('미용 중이면 날짜·시간·반려동물은 막고, 메뉴는 고쳐 같은 시각으로 보낸다', (tester) async {
    final backend = _backend(status: 'in_progress', group: false);
    Map<String, dynamic>? sent;
    backend.routes['PUT /v1/shops/3/visits/101'] = (o, body) {
      sent = bodyOf(body);
      return FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: {'visit': visitJson(), 'warnings': <String>[]},
      );
    };
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    AppChoiceChip chip(String label) => tester.widget<AppChoiceChip>(
      find.byWidgetPredicate((w) => w is AppChoiceChip && w.label == label),
    );
    expect(chip('보리').onTap, isNull);
    await tester.tap(find.text('15:00'));
    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel('10월 3일 토'));
    await settle(tester);
    await _tapLabel(tester, '위생');
    await _save(tester);

    expect(sent!['starts_at'], '2026-10-02T14:00:00+09:00');
    expect(sent!['pet_id'], 11);
    expect(sent!['menu_ids'], [1, 2]);
  });

  testWidgets('묶음의 시간을 바꾸면 같이 옮길지 묻고, 같이 옮기면 묶음을 한 번에 옮긴 뒤 이 방문을 고친다', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final backend = _backend();
    Map<String, dynamic>? moved;
    backend.routes['POST /v1/shops/3/visits/reschedule'] = (o, body) {
      moved = bodyOf(body);
      return FakeResponse(statusCode: 200, data: savedJson([visitJson()]));
    };
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await _tapLabel(tester, '16:00');
    await _save(tester);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.moveTogetherQuestion('보리')), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text(AppStrings.moveTogether));
    await settle(tester);

    expect(moved, {
      'visit_ids': [101, 102],
      'starts_at': '2026-10-02T16:00:00+09:00',
    });
    final writes = [
      for (final c in setup.calls)
        if (!c.startsWith('GET')) c,
    ];
    expect(writes, [
      'POST /v1/shops/3/visits/reschedule',
      'PUT /v1/shops/3/visits/101',
    ]);
  });

  testWidgets('이 방문만 옮기면 PUT 하나만 보낸다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await _tapLabel(tester, '16:00');
    await _save(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.text(AppStrings.moveOnlyThis));
    await settle(tester);

    expect(setup.calls, isNot(contains('POST /v1/shops/3/visits/reschedule')));
    expect(setup.calls, contains('PUT /v1/shops/3/visits/101'));
  });

  testWidgets('시간을 그대로 두면 묻지 않는다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await _save(tester);

    expect(find.text(AppStrings.moveTogetherQuestion('보리')), findsNothing);
    expect(setup.calls, contains('PUT /v1/shops/3/visits/101'));
  });

  testWidgets('묶음의 다른 방문이 예약됨이 아니면(완료 등) 묻지 않는다', (tester) async {
    final backend = _backend();
    final dubu = visitJson();
    final bori = visitJson(
      visitId: 102,
      status: 'completed',
      pet: visitPetJson(petId: 12, name: '보리'),
    );
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits/101'] = (o, b) => FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 200,
      data: visitDetailJson(visit: dubu, group: [dubu, bori]),
    );
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await _tapLabel(tester, '16:00');
    await _save(tester);

    expect(find.text(AppStrings.moveTogetherQuestion('보리')), findsNothing);
    expect(setup.calls, contains('PUT /v1/shops/3/visits/101'));
  });

  testWidgets('보관된 메뉴가 붙어 있으면 칩으로 보이고, 그대로 저장하면 남는다', (tester) async {
    final backend = _backend(group: false);
    final dubu = visitJson(menus: [menuBriefJson(menuId: 77, name: '옛 메뉴')]);
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits/101'] = (o, b) => FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 200,
      data: visitDetailJson(visit: dubu, group: [dubu]),
    );
    Map<String, dynamic>? sent;
    backend.routes['PUT /v1/shops/3/visits/101'] = (o, body) {
      sent = bodyOf(body);
      return FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: {'visit': dubu, 'warnings': <String>[]},
      );
    };
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    expect(find.bySemanticsLabel('옛 메뉴'), findsOneWidget);
    await _save(tester);

    expect(sent!['menu_ids'], [77]);
  });

  testWidgets('시간 칸은 같은 묶음의 방문을 빼고 묻는다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    final slotQuery = setup.adapter.requests.firstWhere(
      (r) => r.path == '/v1/shops/3/visits/slots',
    );
    expect(slotQuery.uri.queryParametersAll['exclude'], ['101', '102']);
  });

  testWidgets('서버가 거절하면 문구를 보이고 남는다', (tester) async {
    final backend = _backend(group: false);
    backend.routes['PUT /v1/shops/3/visits/101'] = (o, b) => const FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 409,
      data: {'code': 'VISIT_STATE_CONFLICT', 'message': 'x'},
    );
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    final closed = await _open(tester, setup);

    await _save(tester);

    expect(closed.closed, isFalse);
    expect(find.textContaining('다른 기기에서 먼저'), findsOneWidget);
  });
}
```

`app/lib/features/visit/screens/visit_edit_screen.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../../core/error/error_messages.dart';
import '../../../core/router/screen_keys.dart';
import '../../../design/app_theme.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_spacing.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_typography.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_back_button.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_button.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_card.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_sheet.dart';
import '../../../l10n/app_strings.dart';
import '../../auth/state/session.dart';
import '../../auth/state/session_controller.dart';
import '../api/visit_models.dart';
import '../state/visit_commands.dart';
import '../state/visit_providers.dart';
import 'visit_form.dart';
import 'visit_labels.dart';

/// 방문 수정 — 예약 ②와 같은 폼(설계 9.1). 날짜·시간·반려동물은 예약됨일
/// 때만 바꾸고, 메뉴·소요·메모는 미용 중·완료일 때도 바꾼다(설계 6.2).
/// 묶음 방문의 시간을 바꾸면 같은 묶음의 예약도 같이 옮길지 묻는다.
class VisitEditScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const VisitEditScreen({super.key, required this.visitId});

  final int visitId;

  @override
  ConsumerState<VisitEditScreen> createState() => _VisitEditScreenState();
}

class _VisitEditScreenState extends ConsumerState<VisitEditScreen> {
  bool _busy = false;
  String? _errorText;

  int? get _shopId {
    final session = ref.read(sessionControllerProvider).value;
    return session is SignedIn ? session.selectedShopId : null;
  }

  /// 같이 옮길 수 있는 묶음의 다른 예약(예약됨만 옮길 수 있다).
  List<Visit> _movableSiblings(VisitDetail detail) => [
    for (final v in detail.group)
      if (v.visitId != detail.visit.visitId && v.status == VisitStatus.reserved)
        v,
  ];

  Future<bool?> _askMoveTogether(List<Visit> siblings) {
    return AppSheet.show<bool>(
      context,
      builder: (context) => Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Text(
            AppStrings.moveTogetherQuestion(visitsTitle(siblings)),
            style: AppTypography.body,
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
          AppButton(
            label: AppStrings.moveTogether,
            filled: true,
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),
          AppButton(
            label: AppStrings.moveOnlyThis,
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _save(VisitDetail detail, VisitFormValue value) async {
    final shopId = _shopId;
    if (shopId == null || _busy) return;
    final visit = detail.visit;
    final startsAt = value.startsAt!;
    final siblings = _movableSiblings(detail);
    var together = false;
    // 문자열이 아니라 벽시계로 견준다 — 서버 직렬화 서식이 달라져도 "안 바뀜"을 안다.
    final timeChanged = ShopTime.parse(startsAt).wall != visit.startsAt.wall;
    if (timeChanged && siblings.isNotEmpty) {
      final answer = await _askMoveTogether(siblings);
      if (answer == null || !mounted) return;
      together = answer;
    }
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _errorText = null;
    });
    final commands = ref.read(visitCommandsProvider);
    try {
      // 묶음을 먼저 한 번에 옮기고(전부 아니면 전무), 이 방문의 나머지 칸을 고친다.
      if (together) {
        await commands.reschedule(
          shopId,
          visitIds: [visit.visitId, for (final v in siblings) v.visitId],
          startsAt: startsAt,
        );
      }
      await commands.update(
        shopId,
        visit.visitId,
        VisitUpdate(
          startsAt: startsAt,
          petId: value.petIds.firstOrNull,
          durationMinutes: value.durationMinutes,
          menuIds: value.menuIds,
          memo: value.memo,
        ),
      );
      if (mounted) context.pop();
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _busy = false;
        _errorText = messageFor(e);
      });
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final shopId = _shopId;
    final key = (shopId: shopId ?? 0, visitId: widget.visitId);
    final detail = shopId == null
        ? const AsyncValue<VisitDetail>.loading()
        : ref.watch(visitDetailProvider(key));
    return Scaffold(
      key: ScreenKeys.visitEdit,
      body: AppBackground(
        child: SafeArea(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              const Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                  AppSpacing.lg,
                  AppSpacing.sm,
                  AppSpacing.lg,
                  AppSpacing.sm,
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    AppBackButton(),
                    SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),
                    Text(
                      AppStrings.editVisitTitle,
                      style: AppTypography.titleKo,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Expanded(
                child: detail.when(
                  loading: () =>
                      const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
                  error: (error, _) => Center(
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Text(messageFor(error), style: AppTypography.body),
                        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
                        AppButton(
                          label: AppStrings.retry,
                          onPressed: () =>
                              ref.invalidate(visitDetailProvider(key)),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  data: (detail) => _form(shopId!, detail),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _form(int shopId, VisitDetail detail) {
    final visit = detail.visit;
    return VisitForm(
      shopId: shopId,
      customerId: visit.customer.customerId,
      initial: VisitFormInitial(
        date: visit.startsAt.day,
        petIds: {if (visit.pet != null) visit.pet!.petId},
        ownerOnly: visit.pet == null,
        at: hhmm(visit.startsAt.wall),
        durationMinutes: visit.durationMinutes,
        menus: visit.menus,
        memo: visit.memo,
        prefilled: true,
      ),
      header: AppCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          horizontal: AppSpacing.md,
          vertical: AppSpacing.md,
        ),
        child: SizedBox(
          width: double.infinity,
          child: Text(
            '${ownerLabel(visit.customer)} · ${statusLabel(visit.status)}',
            style: AppTypography.bodyStrong,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      submitLabel: (date, at) => AppStrings.save,
      onSubmit: (value) => _save(detail, value),
      busy: _busy,
      errorText: _errorText,
      singlePet: true,
      scheduleLocked: visit.status != VisitStatus.reserved,
      // 옮기는 동안 자기 자신·같은 묶음과 겹친다고 하지 않게 뺀다.
      excludeVisitIds: [for (final v in detail.group) v.visitId],
    );
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 테스트가 통과하는지 확인한다**

Run (`app/`): `flutter test test/features/visit/screens/visit_edit_screen_test.dart --timeout 20s`
Expected: 10개 통과

Run (`app/`): `flutter analyze` → `No issues found!`, `flutter test --timeout 30s` → 전부 통과(393개).

- [ ] **Step 5: 판별력을 확인한다(변이)**

각 변이를 하나씩 넣고 그 테스트가 실패하는지 본 뒤 되돌린다.

- `scheduleLocked: visit.status != VisitStatus.reserved`를 `false`로 → `미용 중이면 날짜·시간·반려동물은 막고…` 실패.
- `excludeVisitIds`를 빈 목록으로 → `시간 칸은 같은 묶음의 방문을 빼고 묻는다` 실패.
- `timeChanged`(벽시계 비교)를 늘 참으로 → `시간을 그대로 두면 묻지 않는다` 실패.
- `if (together)` 블록을 뺀다 → `묶음의 시간을 바꾸면 같이 옮길지 묻고…` 실패.
- `_movableSiblings`의 `v.status == VisitStatus.reserved`를 뺀다 → `묶음의 다른 방문이 예약됨이 아니면(완료 등) 묻지 않는다` 실패.

- [ ] **Step 6: 커밋한다**

```bash
git add -A
git commit -m "feat(visit): 방문 수정 — 같은 폼, 묶음 같이 옮기기

Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

---

### Task 6: 방문 상세와 상태 시트

`VisitDetailScreen`(`/visits/:visitId` — **`/visits/new` 뒤에** 둔다) 과 공용 흐름 `visit_actions.dart`: 시트 셋(`VisitSheets.cancelReason/confirmNoShow/complete`), 묻기 `askVisitAction`, 보내기 `sendVisitAction`(묶음 한 번에, "완료하고 다음 예약"이면 완료 뒤 미리 받아 둔 초안으로 `/visits/new` extra). 완료 시트는 띄우기 전에 `nextDraft`를 읽어 "→ 10월 30일 금 14:00"과 안내를 보인다(Task 1 의 백엔드 보강이 전제). 버튼은 같은 묶음·같은 상태의 방문을 한 번에 바꾼다(새로 정한 것 10·11, V6·V14·A1·A10).

**Files:**
- Modify: `app/lib/core/router/app_router.dart`
- Modify: `app/lib/core/router/screen_keys.dart`
- Create: `app/lib/features/visit/screens/visit_actions.dart`
- Create: `app/lib/features/visit/screens/visit_detail_screen.dart`
- Modify: `app/lib/l10n/app_strings.dart`
- Modify: `app/test/design/glass_boundary_test.dart`
- Create: `app/test/features/visit/screens/visit_detail_screen_test.dart`
- Golden: `app/test/features/visit/screens/goldens/complete_sheet.png` (이 작업의 골든 테스트가 `--update-goldens`로 만든다)
- Golden: `app/test/features/visit/screens/goldens/visit_detail.png` (이 작업의 골든 테스트가 `--update-goldens`로 만든다)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3 의 `visitDetailProvider`·`VisitCommands.transition/nextDraft`, Task 4 의 라벨 도우미와 `RegisterVisitScreen`(초안으로 열기), `formatWeight`(customer 기능).
- Produces: `VisitSheets`(`complete(context, {draft})`), `VisitChoice{action, cancelReason, thenNext, draft}`, `askVisitAction(context, ref, shopId:, action:, anchorVisitId:) → VisitChoice?`, `sendVisitAction(context, ref, shopId:, choice:, ids:, anchorVisitId:)`, `VisitDetailScreen(visitId)`, `ScreenKeys.visitDetail`, 문구(상태 버튼·시트).

- [ ] **Step 1: 테스트를 쓴다**

아래 테스트 파일을 그대로 만든다(수정하는 테스트는 diff 대로 고친다).

`app/test/features/visit/screens/visit_detail_screen_test.dart`:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:chagocnote/core/router/screen_keys.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/design/widgets/app_button.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/design/widgets/app_card.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/features/home/state/today_provider.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/l10n/app_strings.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import '../../../support/customer_test_app.dart';
import '../../../support/fake_http_client_adapter.dart';
import '../../../support/visit_fixtures.dart';
import '../../../support/visit_test_backend.dart';

final _today = [todayProvider.overrideWithValue(DateTime(2026, 10, 2))];

/// 방문 101(두부)을 [status]로 보이는 서버. 전이를 받으면 상태를 바꿔 다음
/// 조회에 반영한다. [withBori]면 102(보리)가 같은 묶음·같은 상태다.
class _Server {
  _Server({this.status = 'reserved', this.withBori = false}) {
    backend = VisitTestBackend({
      'GET /v1/shops/3/visits/101': (o, b) => FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: visitDetailJson(
          visit: _dubu(),
          group: [_dubu(), if (withBori) _bori()],
        ),
      ),
      'GET /v1/shops/3/visits/102': (o, b) => FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: visitDetailJson(visit: _bori(), group: [_dubu(), _bori()]),
      ),
      'POST /v1/shops/3/visits/transitions': (o, body) {
        final sent = bodyOf(body);
        transitions.add(sent);
        cancelReason = sent['cancel_reason'] as String?;
        status = switch (sent['action']) {
          'start' => 'in_progress',
          'complete' => 'completed',
          'cancel' => 'canceled',
          'no_show' => 'no_show',
          _ => 'reserved',
        };
        return FakeResponse(
          statusCode: 200,
          data: {
            'visits': [_dubu()],
          },
        );
      },
      'GET /v1/shops/3/visits/101/next-draft': (o, b) =>
          FakeResponse(statusCode: 200, data: nextDraftJson()),
    });
  }

  String status;
  String? cancelReason;
  final bool withBori;
  final transitions = <Map<String, dynamic>>[];
  late final VisitTestBackend backend;

  Map<String, dynamic> _dubu() =>
      visitJson(status: status, cancelReason: cancelReason);
  Map<String, dynamic> _bori() => visitJson(
    visitId: 102,
    status: status,
    pet: visitPetJson(petId: 12, name: '보리'),
  );
}

Future<void> _open(WidgetTester tester, CustomerTestSetup setup) async {
  useTallView(tester, height: 1200);
  await pushOnHome<Object?>(tester, setup, '/visits/101');
}

Finder _button(String label) =>
    find.byWidgetPredicate((w) => w is AppButton && w.label == label);

Future<void> _tap(WidgetTester tester, Finder finder) async {
  await tester.tap(finder);
  await settle(tester);
}

Future<void> _more(WidgetTester tester, String label) async {
  await _tap(tester, find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.more));
  await _tap(tester, find.text(label));
}

void main() {
  testWidgets('예약됨이면 날짜·메뉴·메모·지난 방문·노쇼 횟수와 [노쇼 · 취소 · 시작]을 보인다', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final server = _Server();
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.visitDetail), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('두부'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.statusReserved), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('말티푸 · 4.2kg · 이수진'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('10월 2일 금 · 14:00 — 15:30'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('목욕'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('발톱 짧게'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('9월 2일 수 · ${AppStrings.noShowCount(0)}'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(_button(AppStrings.actionNoShow), findsOneWidget);
    expect(_button(AppStrings.actionCancelVisit), findsOneWidget);
    expect(_button(AppStrings.actionStart), findsOneWidget);
    expect(_button(AppStrings.actionComplete), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('시작은 같은 묶음·같은 상태의 방문을 한 번에 보내고, 다시 불러와 미용 중으로 보인다', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final server = _Server(withBori: true);
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.groupLabel), findsOneWidget);

    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.actionStart));

    expect(server.transitions.single, {
      'visit_ids': [101, 102],
      'action': 'start',
    });
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.statusInProgress), findsWidgets);
    expect(_button(AppStrings.actionComplete), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('묶음이라도 상태가 다른 방문은 함께 보내지 않는다', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server();
    server.backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits/101'] = (o, b) {
      final dubu = visitJson();
      final bori = visitJson(
        visitId: 102,
        status: 'canceled',
        pet: visitPetJson(petId: 12, name: '보리'),
      );
      return FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: visitDetailJson(visit: dubu, group: [dubu, bori]),
      );
    };
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.actionStart));

    expect(server.transitions.single['visit_ids'], [101]);
  });

  testWidgets('취소는 사유를 고른 뒤 보내고, 시트를 닫으면 보내지 않는다', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server();
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.actionCancelVisit));
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.cancelReasonTitle), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tapAt(const Offset(10, 10));
    await settle(tester);
    expect(server.transitions, isEmpty);

    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.actionCancelVisit));
    await _tap(tester, find.text(AppStrings.reasonShopReason));

    expect(server.transitions.single, {
      'visit_ids': [101],
      'action': 'cancel',
      'cancel_reason': 'shop_reason',
    });
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.reasonShopReason), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('노쇼는 한 번 묻고 보낸다', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server();
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.actionNoShow));
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.noShowConfirm), findsOneWidget);
    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.actionNoShow).last);

    expect(server.transitions.single['action'], 'no_show');
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.statusNoShow), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('미용 중이면 [완료]만 있고, 완료 시트에서 완료를 고르면 완료한다', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server(status: 'in_progress');
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    expect(_button(AppStrings.actionStart), findsNothing);

    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.actionComplete));
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.completeTitle), findsOneWidget);
    // 시트는 다음 예약이 언제로 잡히는지 미리 보인다(초안을 먼저 읽는다).
    expect(
      find.text(AppStrings.nextPreview('10월 30일 금 14:00')),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.completeAndNextCaption(4)), findsOneWidget);
    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.actionComplete).last);

    expect(server.transitions.single['action'], 'complete');
    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.registerVisit), findsNothing);
    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.visitDetail), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('완료하고 다음 예약 잡기는 완료한 뒤 초안으로 예약 ②를 채워 연다', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server(status: 'in_progress');
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.actionComplete));
    await _tap(tester, find.text(AppStrings.completeAndNext));

    final writes = [
      for (final c in setup.calls)
        if (c.contains('transitions') || c.contains('next-draft')) c,
    ];
    // 초안은 시트를 띄우기 전에 한 번만 읽고, 완료한 뒤 그 초안으로 연다.
    expect(writes, [
      'GET /v1/shops/3/visits/101/next-draft',
      'POST /v1/shops/3/visits/transitions',
    ]);
    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.registerVisit), findsOneWidget);
    expect(
      find.text(AppStrings.saveReservation('10월 30일 금 14:00')),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
  });

  testWidgets('초안을 못 읽어도 완료 시트는 뜨고 완료할 수 있다', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server(status: 'in_progress');
    server.backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits/101/next-draft'] = (o, b) =>
        const FakeResponse(statusCode: 503, data: 'x');
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.actionComplete));
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.completeTitle), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.textContaining('→'), findsNothing);
    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.actionComplete).last);

    expect(server.transitions.single['action'], 'complete');
  });

  testWidgets('··· 에서 바로 완료는 예약됨에서 완료 시트를 거쳐 보낸다', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server();
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await _more(tester, AppStrings.actionCompleteNow);
    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.actionComplete).last);

    expect(server.transitions.single['action'], 'complete');
  });

  testWidgets('완료한 방문은 버튼이 없고 ··· 에 되돌리기·다음 예약 잡기가 있다', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server(status: 'completed');
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    expect(_button(AppStrings.actionComplete), findsNothing);
    expect(_button(AppStrings.actionStart), findsNothing);

    await _tap(tester, find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.more));
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.actionCompleteNow), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.actionNextReservation), findsOneWidget);
    await _tap(tester, find.text(AppStrings.actionRevert));

    expect(server.transitions.single['action'], 'revert');
    expect(_button(AppStrings.actionStart), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('취소한 방문의 ··· 에는 수정이 없고 되돌리기만 있다', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server(status: 'canceled');
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await _tap(tester, find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.more));

    expect(find.text(AppStrings.edit), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.actionRevert), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('··· 의 수정은 수정 화면을 연다', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server();
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await _more(tester, AppStrings.edit);

    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.visitEdit), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('서버가 거절하면(VISIT_NOT_YET) 문구를 보이고, 화면을 다시 불러온다', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server();
    server.backend.routes['POST /v1/shops/3/visits/transitions'] = (o, b) =>
        const FakeResponse(
          statusCode: 409,
          data: {'code': 'VISIT_NOT_YET', 'message': 'x'},
        );
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    setup.adapter.requests.clear();

    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.actionStart));

    expect(find.textContaining('아직 할 수 없습니다'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(setup.calls, contains('GET /v1/shops/3/visits/101'));
  });

  testWidgets('보내는 동안 버튼과 ··· 를 막아 한 번만 보낸다', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server();
    final gate = Completer<FakeResponse>();
    server.backend.routes['POST /v1/shops/3/visits/transitions'] = (o, b) =>
        gate.future;
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await tester.tap(_button(AppStrings.actionStart));
    await tester.pump();
    expect(
      tester.widget<AppButton>(_button(AppStrings.actionNoShow)).onPressed,
      isNull,
    );
    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.more));
    await tester.pump();
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.actionCompleteNow), findsNothing);
    gate.complete(
      FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: {
          'visits': [visitJson(status: 'in_progress')],
        },
      ),
    );
    await settle(tester);

    expect(setup.calls.where((c) => c.endsWith('/transitions')), hasLength(1));
  });

  testWidgets('함께 온 아이를 누르면 그 방문으로 바꿔 끼운다', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server(withBori: true);
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await _tap(tester, find.text('보리'));

    expect(setup.calls, contains('GET /v1/shops/3/visits/102'));
    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.visitDetail), findsOneWidget);
    await _tap(tester, find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.back));
    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.visitDetail), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('익명화한 고객의 방문은 "삭제된 고객"으로 보인다', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server();
    server.backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits/101'] = (o, b) {
      final v = visitJson(
        customer: visitCustomerJson(name: null, phoneMasked: null),
        pet: visitPetJson(name: null),
        status: 'completed',
      );
      return FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: visitDetailJson(visit: v, previousVisit: null),
      );
    };
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    expect(
      find.text('말티푸 · 4.2kg · ${AppStrings.deletedCustomer}'),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(
      find.text('${AppStrings.none} · ${AppStrings.noShowCount(0)}'),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
  });

  testWidgets('불러오지 못하면 다시 시도할 수 있고 뒤로 갈 수 있다', (tester) async {
    var fail = true;
    final server = _Server();
    final ok = server.backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits/101']!;
    server.backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits/101'] = (o, b) => fail
        ? const FakeResponse(
            statusCode: 404,
            data: {'code': 'VISIT_NOT_FOUND', 'message': 'x'},
          )
        : ok(o, b);
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    expect(find.textContaining('방문을 찾을 수 없습니다'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.back), findsOneWidget);

    fail = false;
    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.retry));

    expect(find.text('두부'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('방문 상세 골든', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server(withBori: true);
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(390, 1000);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();

    await expectLater(
      find.byKey(ScreenKeys.visitDetail),
      matchesGoldenFile('goldens/visit_detail.png'),
    );
  });

  testWidgets('완료 시트 골든', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server(status: 'in_progress');
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(server.backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(390, 844);
    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.actionComplete));

    await expectLater(
      find
          .ancestor(
            of: find.text(AppStrings.completeTitle),
            matching: find.byType(AppCard),
          )
          .first,
      matchesGoldenFile('goldens/complete_sheet.png'),
    );
  });
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../../design/tokens/app_spacing.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_typography.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_button.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_sheet.dart';
import '../../../l10n/app_strings.dart';
import '../api/visit_models.dart';
import '../state/visit_commands.dart';
import 'visit_labels.dart';

/// 상태를 바꾸기 전에 묻는 시트들 — 방문 상세와 하루 목록 카드가 함께 쓴다.
abstract final class VisitSheets {
  /// 취소 사유(필수). 닫으면 null.
  static Future<String?> cancelReason(BuildContext context) =>
      AppSheet.show<String>(
        context,
        builder: (context) => Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const Text(AppStrings.cancelReasonTitle, style: AppTypography.body),
            const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
            AppButton(
              label: AppStrings.reasonCustomerRequest,
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop('customer_request'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),
            AppButton(
              label: AppStrings.reasonShopReason,
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop('shop_reason'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );

  /// 노쇼는 보호자 기록에 남으므로 한 번 묻는다.
  static Future<bool?> confirmNoShow(BuildContext context) =>
      AppSheet.show<bool>(
        context,
        builder: (context) => Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const Text(
              AppStrings.noShowConfirm,
              style: AppTypography.body,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
            AppButton(
              label: AppStrings.actionNoShow,
              filled: true,
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),
            AppButton(
              label: AppStrings.cancel,
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );

  /// 완료 시트. true 면 "완료하고 다음 예약 잡기", false 면 완료만, 닫으면 null.
  /// [draft]가 있으면 다음 예약이 언제로 잡히는지 미리 보인다(목업 ④).
  static Future<bool?> complete(
    BuildContext context, {
    NextVisitDraft? draft,
  }) => AppSheet.show<bool>(
    context,
    builder: (context) => Column(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        const Text(AppStrings.completeTitle, style: AppTypography.titleKo),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
        AppButton(
          label: AppStrings.actionComplete,
          filled: true,
          icon: Icons.check,
          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),
        AppButton(
          label: AppStrings.completeAndNext,
          icon: Icons.arrow_forward,
          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),
        ),
        if (draft != null) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),
          Text(
            AppStrings.nextPreview(
              '${dayLabel(draft.startsAt.wall)} ${hhmm(draft.startsAt.wall)}',
            ),
            style: AppTypography.bodyStrong,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),
          Text(
            AppStrings.completeAndNextCaption(draft.intervalWeeks),
            style: AppTypography.caption,
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          ),
        ],
      ],
    ),
  );
}

/// 시트에서 고른 상태 전이. [cancelReason]은 취소일 때만, [thenNext]는
/// "완료하고 다음 예약 잡기"일 때 true, [draft]는 그때 미리 받아 둔 초안.
class VisitChoice {
  const VisitChoice(
    this.action, {
    this.cancelReason,
    this.thenNext = false,
    this.draft,
  });

  final String action;
  final String? cancelReason;
  final bool thenNext;
  final NextVisitDraft? draft;
}

/// 전이에 필요한 것을 시트로 묻는다(취소 사유, 노쇼 확인, 완료 / 완료하고
/// 다음 예약). 묻지 않는 전이(시작·되돌리기)는 그대로 돌려준다. 시트를 닫으면
/// null — 아무것도 보내지 않는다.
///
/// 완료 시트는 먼저 [anchorVisitId]의 다음 예약 초안을 읽어 날짜를 미리 보인다
/// (백엔드가 완료 전 방문의 초안도 준다). 초안을 못 읽어도 시트는 뜬다 —
/// 미리보기는 돕는 것이다.
///
/// 묻는 것과 보내는 것을 나눈다 — 시트가 떠 있는 동안 화면이 "보내는 중"이
/// 되지 않게.
Future<VisitChoice?> askVisitAction(
  BuildContext context,
  WidgetRef ref, {
  required int shopId,
  required String action,
  required int anchorVisitId,
}) async {
  switch (action) {
    case VisitAction.cancel:
      final reason = await VisitSheets.cancelReason(context);
      return reason == null ? null : VisitChoice(action, cancelReason: reason);
    case VisitAction.noShow:
      final ok = await VisitSheets.confirmNoShow(context);
      return ok == true ? const VisitChoice(VisitAction.noShow) : null;
    case VisitAction.complete:
      NextVisitDraft? draft;
      try {
        draft = await ref
            .read(visitCommandsProvider)
            .nextDraft(shopId, anchorVisitId);
      } catch (_) {
        // 미리보기를 못 받아도 완료는 할 수 있어야 한다.
      }
      if (!context.mounted) return null;
      final next = await VisitSheets.complete(context, draft: draft);
      return next == null
          ? null
          : VisitChoice(VisitAction.complete, thenNext: next, draft: draft);
    default:
      return VisitChoice(action);
  }
}

/// 고른 전이를 묶음 한 번에 보낸다. [ids]는 같은 보호자·같은 상태의 방문들이다
/// (설계 6.2 — 가족 한 번에). "완료하고 다음 예약 잡기"면 완료한 뒤 미리 받아
/// 둔 초안(없으면 [anchorVisitId]로 다시 읽는다)으로 예약 ② 화면을 채워 연다.
Future<void> sendVisitAction(
  BuildContext context,
  WidgetRef ref, {
  required int shopId,
  required VisitChoice choice,
  required List<int> ids,
  required int anchorVisitId,
}) async {
  final commands = ref.read(visitCommandsProvider);
  await commands.transition(
    shopId,
    visitIds: ids,
    action: choice.action,
    cancelReason: choice.cancelReason,
  );
  if (!choice.thenNext) return;
  final draft = choice.draft ?? await commands.nextDraft(shopId, anchorVisitId);
  // 기다리지 않는다 — 예약 화면이 닫힐 때까지 부른 화면이 "보내는 중"에 묶이지 않게.
  if (context.mounted) context.push('/visits/new', extra: draft);
}
```

`app/lib/features/visit/screens/visit_detail_screen.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../../core/error/error_messages.dart';
import '../../../core/router/screen_keys.dart';
import '../../../design/app_theme.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_spacing.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_typography.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_back_button.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_button.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_card.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_chip.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_photo_tile.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_sheet.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/pressable.dart';
import '../../../l10n/app_strings.dart';
import '../../auth/state/session.dart';
import '../../auth/state/session_controller.dart';
import '../../customer/state/customer_input.dart';
import '../api/visit_models.dart';
import '../state/visit_commands.dart';
import '../state/visit_providers.dart';
import 'visit_actions.dart';
import 'visit_labels.dart';

/// 방문 하나 — 사진 자리와 상태, 이름·품종·몸무게·보호자, 날짜·메뉴·메모·
/// 지난 방문·노쇼 횟수, 상태에 맞는 버튼(설계 9.1). `···`에 수정·되돌리기·
/// 바로 완료·다음 예약 잡기가 있다.
///
/// 버튼은 같은 묶음에서 이 방문과 상태가 같은 방문들을 한 번에 바꾼다 —
/// 두 마리가 함께 왔으면 함께 시작하고 함께 끝낸다.
class VisitDetailScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const VisitDetailScreen({super.key, required this.visitId});

  final int visitId;

  @override
  ConsumerState<VisitDetailScreen> createState() => _VisitDetailScreenState();
}

class _VisitDetailScreenState extends ConsumerState<VisitDetailScreen> {
  bool _busy = false;
  String? _errorText;

  int? get _shopId {
    final session = ref.read(sessionControllerProvider).value;
    return session is SignedIn ? session.selectedShopId : null;
  }

  /// 같은 묶음에서 이 방문과 상태가 같은 방문(자기 포함).
  List<int> _sameStatusIds(VisitDetail detail) => [
    for (final v in detail.group)
      if (v.status == detail.visit.status) v.visitId,
  ];

  Future<void> _act(VisitDetail detail, String action) async {
    final shopId = _shopId;
    if (shopId == null || _busy) return;
    final choice = await askVisitAction(
      context,
      ref,
      shopId: shopId,
      action: action,
      anchorVisitId: detail.visit.visitId,
    );
    if (choice == null || !mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _errorText = null;
    });
    try {
      await sendVisitAction(
        context,
        ref,
        shopId: shopId,
        choice: choice,
        ids: _sameStatusIds(detail),
        anchorVisitId: detail.visit.visitId,
      );
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _busy = false;
        _errorText = messageFor(e);
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _openMore(VisitDetail detail) async {
    final status = detail.visit.status;
    final choice = await AppSheet.show<String>(
      context,
      builder: (context) => Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          if (status != VisitStatus.canceled && status != VisitStatus.noShow)
            const _SheetAction(label: AppStrings.edit, value: 'edit'),
          if (status == VisitStatus.reserved)
            const _SheetAction(
              label: AppStrings.actionCompleteNow,
              value: VisitAction.complete,
            ),
          if (status != VisitStatus.reserved)
            const _SheetAction(
              label: AppStrings.actionRevert,
              value: VisitAction.revert,
            ),
          if (status == VisitStatus.completed)
            const _SheetAction(
              label: AppStrings.actionNextReservation,
              value: 'next',
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (choice == null || !mounted) return;
    switch (choice) {
      case 'edit':
        context.push('/visits/${detail.visit.visitId}/edit');
      case 'next':
        await _openNext(detail);
      default:
        await _act(detail, choice);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _openNext(VisitDetail detail) async {
    final shopId = _shopId;
    if (shopId == null || _busy) return;
    try {
      final draft = await ref
          .read(visitCommandsProvider)
          .nextDraft(shopId, detail.visit.visitId);
      if (mounted) context.push('/visits/new', extra: draft);
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _errorText = messageFor(e));
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final shopId = _shopId;
    final key = (shopId: shopId ?? 0, visitId: widget.visitId);
    final detail = shopId == null
        ? const AsyncValue<VisitDetail>.loading()
        : ref.watch(visitDetailProvider(key));
    final data = detail.value;
    return Scaffold(
      key: ScreenKeys.visitDetail,
      body: AppBackground(
        child: SafeArea(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              // 머리는 로딩·오류 상태에서도 보인다 — 오류에 갇혀도 뒤로 갈 수 있다.
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                  AppSpacing.lg,
                  AppSpacing.sm,
                  AppSpacing.sm,
                  0,
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    const AppBackButton(),
                    const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),
                    const Text(
                      AppStrings.visitTitleEn,
                      style: AppTypography.titleEn,
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                    if (data != null)
                      Pressable(
                        semanticLabel: AppStrings.more,
                        onTap: _busy ? null : () => _openMore(data),
                        child: const Icon(Icons.more_horiz),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Expanded(
                child: detail.when(
                  loading: () =>
                      const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
                  error: (error, _) => Center(
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Text(messageFor(error), style: AppTypography.body),
                        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
                        AppButton(
                          label: AppStrings.retry,
                          onPressed: () =>
                              ref.invalidate(visitDetailProvider(key)),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  data: (detail) => _Body(
                    detail: detail,
                    busy: _busy,
                    errorText: _errorText,
                    onAction: (action) => _act(detail, action),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _Body extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Body({
    required this.detail,
    required this.busy,
    required this.errorText,
    required this.onAction,
  });

  final VisitDetail detail;
  final bool busy;
  final String? errorText;
  final ValueChanged<String> onAction;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final visit = detail.visit;
    final pet = visit.pet;
    final others = [
      for (final v in detail.group)
        if (v.visitId != visit.visitId) v,
    ];
    final previous = detail.previousVisit;
    return ListView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppSpacing.lg),
      children: [
        // 사진은 ④에서 붙인다 — 지금은 회색 자리.
        const Center(child: AppPhotoTile(image: null, size: 160)),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
        Wrap(
          spacing: AppSpacing.sm,
          crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
          children: [
            Text(visitsTitle([visit]), style: AppTypography.titleKo),
            AppChip(label: statusLabel(visit.status)),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),
        Text(
          [
            if (pet != null) ...[pet.breed, formatWeight(pet.weightKg)],
            ownerLabel(visit.customer),
          ].join(' · '),
          style: AppTypography.caption,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
        _InfoCard(
          label: AppStrings.visitDateLabel,
          value:
              '${dayLabel(visit.startsAt.wall)} · ${hhmm(visit.startsAt.wall)} — ${hhmm(visit.endsAt.wall)}',
        ),
        _InfoCard(label: AppStrings.menuLabel, value: menusLabel(visit.menus)),
        if (visit.memo != null)
          _InfoCard(label: AppStrings.visitMemoLabel, value: visit.memo!),
        if (visit.cancelReason != null)
          _InfoCard(
            label: AppStrings.cancelReasonLabel,
            value: visit.cancelReason == 'shop_reason'
                ? AppStrings.reasonShopReason
                : AppStrings.reasonCustomerRequest,
          ),
        _InfoCard(
          label: AppStrings.previousVisitLabel,
          value:
              '${previous == null ? AppStrings.none : dayLabel(previous.startsAt.wall)} · ${AppStrings.noShowCount(detail.noShowCount)}',
        ),
        if (others.isNotEmpty) _GroupCard(others: others),
        if (errorText != null)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: AppSpacing.sm),
            child: Text(errorText!, style: AppTypography.caption),
          ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),
        _StatusButtons(status: visit.status, busy: busy, onAction: onAction),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// 상태에 맞는 버튼만 — 예약됨이면 [노쇼 · 취소 · 시작], 미용 중이면 [완료].
class _StatusButtons extends StatelessWidget {
  const _StatusButtons({
    required this.status,
    required this.busy,
    required this.onAction,
  });

  final String status;
  final bool busy;
  final ValueChanged<String> onAction;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    VoidCallback? on(String action) => busy ? null : () => onAction(action);
    return switch (status) {
      VisitStatus.reserved => Row(
        children: [
          AppButton(
            label: AppStrings.actionNoShow,
            onPressed: on(VisitAction.noShow),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),
          AppButton(
            label: AppStrings.actionCancelVisit,
            onPressed: on(VisitAction.cancel),
          ),
          const Spacer(),
          AppButton(
            label: AppStrings.actionStart,
            filled: true,
            busy: busy,
            onPressed: on(VisitAction.start),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      VisitStatus.inProgress => AppButton(
        label: AppStrings.actionComplete,
        filled: true,
        busy: busy,
        onPressed: on(VisitAction.complete),
      ),
      _ => const SizedBox.shrink(),
    };
  }
}

class _InfoCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _InfoCard({required this.label, required this.value});

  final String label;
  final String value;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.sm),
      child: SizedBox(
        width: double.infinity,
        child: AppCard(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(label, style: AppTypography.caption),
              const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),
              Text(value, style: AppTypography.body),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 같은 묶음의 다른 방문. 누르면 그 방문으로 화면을 바꿔 끼운다.
class _GroupCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _GroupCard({required this.others});

  final List<Visit> others;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: double.infinity,
      child: AppCard(
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const Text(AppStrings.groupLabel, style: AppTypography.caption),
            for (final v in others)
              Pressable(
                semanticLabel: visitsTitle([v]),
                onTap: () => context.pushReplacement('/visits/${v.visitId}'),
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppSpacing.sm),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      Text(visitsTitle([v]), style: AppTypography.bodyStrong),
                      const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),
                      AppChip(label: statusLabel(v.status)),
                      const Spacer(),
                      const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SheetAction extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SheetAction({required this.label, required this.value});

  final String label;
  final String value;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Pressable(
      onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(value),
      semanticLabel: label,
      child: Container(
        width: double.infinity,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppSpacing.md),
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        child: Text(label, style: AppTypography.body),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 골든을 만든다**

Run (`app/`): `flutter test test/features/visit/screens/visit_detail_screen_test.dart --update-goldens --timeout 20s`
Expected: 골든 파일이 생긴다(`complete_sheet.png`, `visit_detail.png`). 눈으로 열어 목업과 견준다. **기존 골든이 이 작업의 의도 밖에서 바뀌면 멈추고 보고한다.**

- [ ] **Step 5: 테스트가 통과하는지 확인한다**

Run (`app/`): `flutter test test/features/visit/screens/visit_detail_screen_test.dart --timeout 20s`
Expected: 19개 통과(골든 2 포함)

Run (`app/`): `flutter analyze` → `No issues found!`, `flutter test --timeout 30s` → 전부 통과(412개).

- [ ] **Step 6: 판별력을 확인한다(변이)**

각 변이를 하나씩 넣고 그 테스트가 실패하는지 본 뒤 되돌린다.

- `_sameStatusIds`를 "자기만"으로 → `시작은 같은 묶음·같은 상태의 방문을 한 번에 보내고…` 실패. "묶음 전부"로 → `묶음이라도 상태가 다른 방문은 함께 보내지 않는다` 실패.
- 머리의 `···`에서 `_busy ? null :`을 뺀다 → `보내는 동안 버튼과 ··· 를 막아 한 번만 보낸다` 실패.
- `askVisitAction`의 노쇼 확인을 뺀다 → `노쇼는 한 번 묻고 보낸다` 실패.
- `sendVisitAction`의 `if (!choice.thenNext) return;` 뒤를 뺀다 → `완료하고 다음 예약 잡기는…` 실패.
- `···`의 수정 항목 조건(취소·노쇼 제외)을 `true`로 → `취소한 방문의 ··· 에는 수정이 없고 되돌리기만 있다` 실패.
- `askVisitAction`의 완료 분기에서 `try/catch`를 빼 초안 실패를 던지게 → `초안을 못 읽어도 완료 시트는 뜨고 완료할 수 있다` 실패.
- `sendVisitAction`에서 `choice.draft ??`를 빼고 늘 다시 읽게 → `완료하고 다음 예약 잡기는…`의 요청 순서(초안 한 번) 실패.

- [ ] **Step 7: 커밋한다**

```bash
git add -A
git commit -m "feat(visit): 방문 상세와 상태 시트 — 취소 사유, 노쇼 확인, 완료하고 다음 예약

Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

---

### Task 7: 하루 화면 — 목록·시간표

`DayScreen`(`/visits/day/:date`) 과 순수 함수 `day_layout.dart`(`groupDay`·`gapsBefore`·`timelineColumns`). 목록은 묶음 카드(시간·끝·겹친 사진 자리·이름·메뉴·보호자·겹침/정리 안 됨 표시·상태 버튼)와 "— N시간 비어 있음 —", 시간표는 시간 줄 위의 블록(겹치면 옆 칸). 보기 방식은 `AppIconToggle`로 바꾸고 기기에 기억한다. 머리는 큰 날짜 + 영문 요일 + "2026년 9월"(`AppBigDate.weekdayName`). 사장에게는 `···`가 있어 이 날을 임시 휴무로 표시하거나 해제한다(새로 정한 것 12·13·15·20, A5·A11).

**Files:**
- Modify: `app/lib/core/router/app_router.dart`
- Modify: `app/lib/core/router/screen_keys.dart`
- Modify: `app/lib/design/widgets/app_big_date.dart`
- Create: `app/lib/features/visit/screens/day_screen.dart`
- Create: `app/lib/features/visit/state/day_layout.dart`
- Modify: `app/lib/l10n/app_strings.dart`
- Modify: `app/test/design/glass_boundary_test.dart`
- Create: `app/test/features/visit/screens/day_screen_test.dart`
- Create: `app/test/features/visit/state/day_layout_test.dart`
- Golden: `app/test/features/visit/screens/goldens/day_list.png` (이 작업의 골든 테스트가 `--update-goldens`로 만든다)
- Golden: `app/test/features/visit/screens/goldens/day_timeline.png` (이 작업의 골든 테스트가 `--update-goldens`로 만든다)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3 의 `dayVisitsProvider`·`visitSettingsProvider`·`dayViewModeProvider`, Task 6 의 `askVisitAction/sendVisitAction`, Task 4 의 라벨 도우미·`RegisterVisitScreen`(+ 로 열기).
- Produces: `DayGroup{items, visits, ids, first, startsAt, endsAt, overlapping, unresolved, occupiesTime, uniformStatus}`, `groupDay(items)`, `gapsBefore(groups, {minMinutes})`, `timelineColumns(groups)`, `DayScreen(date)`, `ScreenKeys.day`, 라우트 `/visits/day/:date`, 문구(하루 화면 묶음 — `dayCountsText`·`gapText`·`yearMonth` 등).

- [ ] **Step 1: 테스트를 쓴다**

아래 테스트 파일을 그대로 만든다(수정하는 테스트는 diff 대로 고친다).

`app/test/features/visit/screens/day_screen_test.dart`:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:chagocnote/core/router/screen_keys.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/design/widgets/app_button.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/features/home/state/today_provider.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/l10n/app_strings.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import '../../../support/customer_test_app.dart';
import '../../../support/fake_http_client_adapter.dart';
import '../../../support/visit_fixtures.dart';
import '../../../support/visit_test_backend.dart';

final _today = [todayProvider.overrideWithValue(DateTime(2026, 9, 23))];

const _date = '2026-09-23';

Map<String, dynamic> _v(
  int id,
  String start, {
  int duration = 60,
  String status = 'reserved',
  String pet = '콩이',
  int petId = 11,
  String owner = '김민지',
  int customerId = 7,
  List<Map<String, dynamic>>? menus,
}) => visitJson(
  visitId: id,
  date: _date,
  start: start,
  durationMinutes: duration,
  status: status,
  pet: visitPetJson(petId: petId, name: pet),
  customer: visitCustomerJson(customerId: customerId, name: owner),
  menus: menus,
);

/// 목업의 하루 — 콩이 완료, 두부·보리 미용 중(묶음), 1시간 빈 뒤 몽이 예약,
/// 겹치는 코코 예약.
List<Map<String, dynamic>> _mockupItems({String duoStatus = 'in_progress'}) => [
  dayItemJson(
    _v(
      1,
      '10:00',
      duration: 150,
      status: 'completed',
      menus: [menuBriefJson(menuId: 4, name: '전체 미용')],
    ),
  ),
  dayItemJson(
    _v(
      2,
      '13:00',
      duration: 90,
      status: duoStatus,
      pet: '두부',
      owner: '이수진',
      customerId: 8,
    ),
    groupKey: 'duo',
  ),
  dayItemJson(
    _v(
      3,
      '13:00',
      duration: 90,
      status: duoStatus,
      pet: '보리',
      petId: 12,
      owner: '이수진',
      customerId: 8,
    ),
    groupKey: 'duo',
  ),
  dayItemJson(
    _v(
      4,
      '15:30',
      duration: 120,
      pet: '몽이',
      owner: '박하늘',
      customerId: 9,
      menus: [menuBriefJson(menuId: 2, name: '위생')],
    ),
    overlapping: true,
  ),
  dayItemJson(
    _v(5, '17:00', pet: '코코', owner: '최유리', customerId: 10, menus: []),
    overlapping: true,
  ),
];

VisitTestBackend _backend([List<Map<String, dynamic>>? items]) =>
    VisitTestBackend({
      'GET /v1/shops/3/visits': (o, b) => FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: dayJson(date: _date, items: items ?? _mockupItems()),
      ),
      'POST /v1/shops/3/visits/transitions': (o, b) => FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: {
          'visits': [_v(2, '13:00', status: 'completed')],
        },
      ),
      'GET /v1/shops/3/visits/2': (o, b) => FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: visitDetailJson(visit: _v(2, '13:00', pet: '두부')),
      ),
      'GET /v1/shops/3/visits/2/next-draft': (o, b) => FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: nextDraftJson(
          petIds: [11, 12],
          startsAt: '2026-10-21T13:00:00+09:00',
        ),
      ),
      'POST /v1/shops/3/closed-dates': (o, b) =>
          FakeResponse(statusCode: 200, data: {'date': _date, 'note': null}),
      'DELETE /v1/shops/3/closed-dates/$_date': (o, b) =>
          const FakeResponse(statusCode: 204),
    });

Future<void> _open(WidgetTester tester, CustomerTestSetup setup) async {
  useTallView(tester, height: 1100);
  await pushOnHome<Object?>(tester, setup, '/visits/day/$_date');
}

Finder _button(String label) =>
    find.byWidgetPredicate((w) => w is AppButton && w.label == label);

void main() {
  testWidgets('묶음을 카드 하나로, 상태에 맞는 버튼과 빈 시간·겹침 표시를 보인다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.day), findsOneWidget);
    expect(
      setup.adapter.requests
          .firstWhere((r) => r.path == '/v1/shops/3/visits')
          .queryParameters,
      {'date': _date},
    );
    expect(find.text('5마리 · 완료 1 · 미용 중 2 · 예약 2'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('두부 · 보리'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('~14:30'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('목욕 · 이수진'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.gapText(60)), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('위생 · 박하늘 · ${AppStrings.markOverlap}'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(
      find.text('${AppStrings.noMenu} · 최유리 · ${AppStrings.markOverlap}'),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(_button(AppStrings.actionCompleteShort), findsOneWidget);
    expect(_button(AppStrings.actionStart), findsNWidgets(2));
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.statusCompleted), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('카드의 [시작]은 묶음 전체를 한 번에 보내고 그날 목록을 다시 불러온다', (tester) async {
    final backend = _backend(_mockupItems(duoStatus: 'reserved'));
    Map<String, dynamic>? sent;
    backend.routes['POST /v1/shops/3/visits/transitions'] = (o, body) {
      sent = bodyOf(body);
      return FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: {
          'visits': [_v(2, '13:00', status: 'in_progress')],
        },
      );
    };
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    setup.adapter.requests.clear();

    await tester.tap(_button(AppStrings.actionStart).first);
    await settle(tester);

    expect(sent, {
      'visit_ids': [2, 3],
      'action': 'start',
    });
    expect(setup.calls, contains('GET /v1/shops/3/visits'));
  });

  testWidgets('카드의 [완료하기]는 완료 시트를 거쳐 보낸다', (tester) async {
    final backend = _backend();
    Map<String, dynamic>? sent;
    backend.routes['POST /v1/shops/3/visits/transitions'] = (o, body) {
      sent = bodyOf(body);
      return FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: {
          'visits': [_v(2, '13:00', status: 'completed')],
        },
      );
    };
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await tester.tap(_button(AppStrings.actionCompleteShort));
    await settle(tester);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.completeTitle), findsOneWidget);
    expect(
      find.text(AppStrings.nextPreview('10월 21일 수 13:00')),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    await tester.tap(_button(AppStrings.actionComplete));
    await settle(tester);

    expect(sent, {
      'visit_ids': [2, 3],
      'action': 'complete',
    });
  });

  testWidgets('한 카드를 보내는 동안 다른 카드의 버튼도 막는다', (tester) async {
    final backend = _backend(_mockupItems(duoStatus: 'reserved'));
    final gate = Completer<FakeResponse>();
    backend.routes['POST /v1/shops/3/visits/transitions'] = (o, b) =>
        gate.future;
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await tester.tap(_button(AppStrings.actionStart).first);
    await tester.pump();

    final buttons = tester.widgetList<AppButton>(
      _button(AppStrings.actionStart),
    );
    expect(buttons.every((b) => b.onPressed == null), isTrue);
    gate.complete(
      FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: {
          'visits': [_v(2, '13:00', status: 'in_progress')],
        },
      ),
    );
    await settle(tester);
  });

  testWidgets('서버가 거절하면 문구를 보인다', (tester) async {
    final backend = _backend(_mockupItems(duoStatus: 'reserved'));
    backend.routes['POST /v1/shops/3/visits/transitions'] = (o, b) =>
        const FakeResponse(
          statusCode: 409,
          data: {'code': 'VISIT_STATE_CONFLICT', 'message': 'x'},
        );
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await tester.tap(_button(AppStrings.actionStart).first);
    await settle(tester);

    expect(find.textContaining('다른 기기에서 먼저'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('카드를 누르면 묶음의 첫 방문 상세로 간다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await tester.tap(find.text('두부 · 보리'));
    await settle(tester);

    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.visitDetail), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('정리 안 된 예약과 취소는 표시하고, 취소는 빈 시간으로 센다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(
      _backend([
        dayItemJson(_v(1, '10:00'), unresolved: true),
        dayItemJson(
          _v(2, '11:00', status: 'canceled', pet: '두부', customerId: 8),
        ),
        dayItemJson(_v(3, '12:00', pet: '몽이', customerId: 9)),
      ]).call,
      overrides: _today,
    );
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    expect(
      find.text('목욕 · 김민지 · ${AppStrings.markUnresolved}'),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.statusCanceled), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.gapText(60)), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('2마리 · 예약 2 · 취소 1'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('시간표로 바꾸면 기기에 기억하고 시간 줄 위에 묶음을 그린다(취소는 그리지 않는다)', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(
      _backend([
        ..._mockupItems(),
        dayItemJson(
          _v(6, '11:00', status: 'canceled', pet: '별이', customerId: 11),
        ),
      ]).call,
      overrides: _today,
    );
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.viewAsTimeline));
    await settle(tester);

    expect(await setup.prefs.readDayViewMode(), 'timeline');
    expect(find.text('10:00'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('19:00'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Wednesday'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('두부 · 보리'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('별이'), findsNothing);
    expect(_button(AppStrings.actionStart), findsNothing);
    // 겹치는 몽이·코코는 옆 칸이다.
    final mong = tester.getRect(find.text('몽이'));
    final coco = tester.getRect(find.text('코코'));
    expect(coco.left, greaterThan(mong.left));
  });

  testWidgets('기억한 것이 시간표면 시간표로 연다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await setup.prefs.writeDayViewMode('timeline');
    await _open(tester, setup);

    expect(find.text('10:00'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(_button(AppStrings.actionStart), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('사장은 ··· 로 이 날을 휴무로 표시하고 해제한다', (tester) async {
    var closed = false;
    final backend = _backend([]);
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits'] = (o, b) => FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 200,
      data: dayJson(date: _date, closedDay: closed),
    );
    backend.routes['POST /v1/shops/3/closed-dates'] = (o, b) {
      closed = true;
      return FakeResponse(statusCode: 200, data: {'date': _date, 'note': null});
    };
    backend.routes['DELETE /v1/shops/3/closed-dates/$_date'] = (o, b) {
      closed = false;
      return const FakeResponse(statusCode: 204);
    };
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.more));
    await settle(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.text(AppStrings.markClosedDate));
    await settle(tester);
    expect(setup.calls, contains('POST /v1/shops/3/closed-dates'));
    expect(find.text('${AppStrings.closedDayNote} · 0마리'), findsOneWidget);

    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.more));
    await settle(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.text(AppStrings.unmarkClosedDate));
    await settle(tester);
    expect(setup.calls, contains('DELETE /v1/shops/3/closed-dates/$_date'));
    expect(find.text('0마리'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('직원에게는 ··· 가 없다(V8)', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(
      _backend().call,
      role: 'staff',
      overrides: _today,
    );
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    expect(find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.more), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('예약이 없으면 안내하고, 휴무일은 머리에 적는다', (tester) async {
    final backend = _backend();
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits'] = (o, b) => FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 200,
      data: dayJson(date: _date, closedDay: true),
    );
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    expect(find.text(AppStrings.dayEmpty), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('${AppStrings.closedDayNote} · 0마리'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('+ 는 그 날짜로 예약 등록을 연다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);

    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.addReservation));
    await settle(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.text('이수진 · 010-12··-5678'));
    await settle(tester);

    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.registerVisit), findsOneWidget);
    final slotDates = {
      for (final r in setup.adapter.requests)
        if (r.path.endsWith('/slots')) r.queryParameters['date'],
    };
    expect(slotDates, {_date});
  });

  testWidgets('불러오지 못하면 다시 시도한다', (tester) async {
    var fail = true;
    final backend = _backend();
    final ok = backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits']!;
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits'] = (o, b) =>
        fail ? const FakeResponse(statusCode: 503, data: 'x') : ok(o, b);
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    expect(_button(AppStrings.retry), findsOneWidget);

    fail = false;
    await tester.tap(_button(AppStrings.retry));
    await settle(tester);

    expect(find.text('두부 · 보리'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('하루 목록 골든', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _open(tester, setup);
    tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(390, 844);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();

    await expectLater(
      find.byKey(ScreenKeys.day),
      matchesGoldenFile('goldens/day_list.png'),
    );
  });

  testWidgets('하루 시간표 골든', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await setup.prefs.writeDayViewMode('timeline');
    await _open(tester, setup);
    tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(390, 844);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();

    await expectLater(
      find.byKey(ScreenKeys.day),
      matchesGoldenFile('goldens/day_timeline.png'),
    );
  });
}
```

```dart
import 'package:chagocnote/features/visit/api/visit_models.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/features/visit/state/day_layout.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import '../../../support/visit_fixtures.dart';

DayVisitItem _item(
  int id,
  String start, {
  int duration = 60,
  String? group,
  String status = 'reserved',
}) => DayVisitItem.fromJson(
  dayItemJson(
    visitJson(
      visitId: id,
      start: start,
      durationMinutes: duration,
      status: status,
    ),
    groupKey: group ?? 'g$id',
  ),
);

void main() {
  test('같은 group_key 는 사이에 다른 방문이 끼어도 한 묶음이다', () {
    final groups = groupDay([
      _item(1, '14:00', group: 'a'),
      _item(2, '14:00', group: 'b'),
      _item(3, '14:00', group: 'a'),
    ]);

    expect(groups.map((g) => g.ids), [
      [1, 3],
      [2],
    ]);
  });

  test('묶음의 끝은 가장 늦게 끝나는 방문이고, 상태가 섞이면 하나로 말하지 않는다', () {
    final group = groupDay([
      _item(1, '13:00', duration: 60, group: 'a'),
      _item(2, '13:00', duration: 90, group: 'a', status: 'in_progress'),
    ]).single;

    expect(group.endsAt, DateTime(2026, 10, 2, 14, 30));
    expect(group.uniformStatus, isNull);
  });

  test('시간을 차지하는 묶음 사이가 한 시간 이상 비면 그 길이를 준다', () {
    final groups = groupDay([
      _item(1, '10:00', duration: 150),
      _item(2, '13:00', duration: 90),
      _item(3, '15:30', duration: 60),
      _item(4, '15:40', duration: 60),
    ]);

    expect(gapsBefore(groups), [null, null, 60, null]);
  });

  test('취소·노쇼는 빈 시간으로 센다', () {
    final groups = groupDay([
      _item(1, '10:00'),
      _item(2, '11:00', status: 'canceled'),
      _item(3, '13:00'),
    ]);

    expect(gapsBefore(groups), [null, null, 120]);
  });

  test('앞 방문이 길게 이어지면 그 끝부터 잰다', () {
    final groups = groupDay([
      _item(1, '10:00', duration: 300),
      _item(2, '11:00'),
      _item(3, '16:00'),
    ]);

    expect(gapsBefore(groups), [null, null, 60]);
  });

  test('겹치는 묶음은 옆 칸으로, 끝난 칸은 다시 쓴다', () {
    final groups = groupDay([
      _item(1, '10:00', duration: 120),
      _item(2, '11:00', duration: 60),
      _item(3, '12:00', duration: 60),
      _item(4, '12:30', duration: 60),
    ]);

    expect(timelineColumns(groups), [0, 1, 0, 1]);
  });
}
```
