`app/lib/features/visit/screens/day_screen.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../../core/error/error_messages.dart';
import '../../../core/router/screen_keys.dart';
import '../../../design/app_theme.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_radius.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_shadows.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_spacing.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_typography.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_back_button.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_big_date.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_button.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_card.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_chip.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_fab.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_icon_toggle.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_photo_tile.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_sheet.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/pressable.dart';
import '../../../l10n/app_strings.dart';
import '../../auth/state/session.dart';
import '../../auth/state/session_controller.dart';
import '../api/visit_models.dart';
import '../state/day_layout.dart';
import '../state/day_view_mode.dart';
import '../state/visit_commands.dart';
import '../state/visit_providers.dart';
import 'visit_actions.dart';
import 'visit_labels.dart';

/// 하루 화면 — 큰 날짜, 상태별 수, [목록 / 시간표] 전환(마지막 선택을 기기에
/// 기억), 가족 묶음 카드(설계 9.1). 목록 카드의 버튼은 묶음 전체를 한 번에
/// 바꾼다 — 예약됨이면 [시작], 미용 중이면 [완료하기].
class DayScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const DayScreen({super.key, required this.date});

  final DateTime date;

  @override
  ConsumerState<DayScreen> createState() => _DayScreenState();
}

class _DayScreenState extends ConsumerState<DayScreen> {
  /// 보내는 중인 묶음의 첫 방문 ID(휴무 표시 중이면 -1). 그동안 다른 버튼을 막는다.
  int? _busyVisitId;
  String? _errorText;

  int? get _shopId {
    final session = ref.read(sessionControllerProvider).value;
    return session is SignedIn ? session.selectedShopId : null;
  }

  Future<void> _act(DayGroup group, String action) async {
    final shopId = _shopId;
    if (shopId == null || _busyVisitId != null) return;
    final choice = await askVisitAction(
      context,
      ref,
      shopId: shopId,
      action: action,
      anchorVisitId: group.first.visitId,
    );
    if (choice == null || !mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _busyVisitId = group.first.visitId;
      _errorText = null;
    });
    try {
      await sendVisitAction(
        context,
        ref,
        shopId: shopId,
        choice: choice,
        ids: group.ids,
        anchorVisitId: group.first.visitId,
      );
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _errorText = messageFor(e));
    }
    if (mounted) setState(() => _busyVisitId = null);
  }

  /// `···` — 이 날을 임시 휴무로 표시하거나 해제한다(사장만, 같은 API 를 쓰는
  /// 설정 화면보다 달력에서 바로 하는 편이 쉽다). 매주 쉬는 요일은 설정에서 푼다.
  Future<void> _openMore(DayVisits data) async {
    final shopId = _shopId;
    if (shopId == null) return;
    final choice = await AppSheet.show<String>(
      context,
      builder: (context) => Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Pressable(
            semanticLabel: data.closedDay
                ? AppStrings.unmarkClosedDate
                : AppStrings.markClosedDate,
            onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop('toggle'),
            child: Container(
              width: double.infinity,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppSpacing.md),
              alignment: Alignment.center,
              child: ExcludeSemantics(
                child: Text(
                  data.closedDay
                      ? AppStrings.unmarkClosedDate
                      : AppStrings.markClosedDate,
                  style: AppTypography.body,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (choice == null || !mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _busyVisitId = -1;
      _errorText = null;
    });
    try {
      final commands = ref.read(visitCommandsProvider);
      if (data.closedDay) {
        await commands.deleteClosedDate(shopId, widget.date);
      } else {
        await commands.addClosedDate(shopId, widget.date);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _errorText = messageFor(e));
    }
    if (mounted) setState(() => _busyVisitId = null);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final shopId = _shopId;
    final key = (shopId: shopId ?? 0, date: widget.date);
    final day = shopId == null
        ? const AsyncValue<DayVisits>.loading()
        : ref.watch(dayVisitsProvider(key));
    final mode = ref.watch(dayViewModeProvider).value ?? DayViewMode.list;
    final data = day.value;
    final session = ref.watch(sessionControllerProvider).value;
    final isOwner =
        session is SignedIn && (session.selectedShop?.isOwner ?? false);
    return Scaffold(
      key: ScreenKeys.day,
      floatingActionButton: AppFab(
        semanticLabel: AppStrings.addReservation,
        onPressed: () =>
            context.push('/visits/new?date=${isoDay(widget.date)}'),
      ),
      body: AppBackground(
        child: SafeArea(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                  AppSpacing.lg,
                  AppSpacing.sm,
                  AppSpacing.lg,
                  0,
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    const AppBackButton(),
                    const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),
                    const Text(
                      AppStrings.dayTitleEn,
                      style: AppTypography.titleEn,
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                    if (isOwner)
                      Pressable(
                        semanticLabel: AppStrings.more,
                        onTap: data == null || _busyVisitId != null
                            ? null
                            : () => _openMore(data),
                        child: const Icon(Icons.more_horiz),
                      ),
                    AppIconToggle(
                      icons: const [Icons.view_list, Icons.view_timeline],
                      labels: const [
                        AppStrings.viewAsList,
                        AppStrings.viewAsTimeline,
                      ],
                      currentIndex: mode.index,
                      onChanged: (i) => ref
                          .read(dayViewModeProvider.notifier)
                          .select(DayViewMode.values[i]),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                  AppSpacing.lg,
                  AppSpacing.md,
                  AppSpacing.lg,
                  AppSpacing.sm,
                ),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    // 목업: 큰 날짜 + 기울인 영문 요일, 그 아래 "2026년 9월".
                    Row(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          '${widget.date.day}',
                          style: AppTypography.bigNumeral,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),
                        Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.xs),
                          child: Text(
                            AppBigDate.weekdayName(widget.date.weekday),
                            style: AppTypography.italicLabel,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),
                    Text(
                      AppStrings.yearMonth(widget.date.year, widget.date.month),
                      style: AppTypography.caption,
                    ),
                    if (data != null) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),
                      Text(
                        [
                          if (data.closedDay) AppStrings.closedDayNote,
                          AppStrings.dayCountsText(
                            visits: data.counts.visitCount,
                            completed: data.counts.completed,
                            inProgress: data.counts.inProgress,
                            reserved: data.counts.reserved,
                            canceled: data.counts.canceled,
                            noShow: data.counts.noShow,
                          ),
                        ].join(' · '),
                        style: AppTypography.bodyStrong,
                      ),
                    ],
                    if (_errorText != null)
                      Text(_errorText!, style: AppTypography.caption),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Expanded(
                child: day.when(
                  loading: () =>
                      const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
                  error: (error, _) => Center(
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Text(messageFor(error), style: AppTypography.body),
                        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
                        AppButton(
                          label: AppStrings.retry,
                          onPressed: () =>
                              ref.invalidate(dayVisitsProvider(key)),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  data: (data) {
                    final groups = groupDay(data.items);
                    if (groups.isEmpty) return const _Empty();
                    return mode == DayViewMode.list
                        ? _DayList(
                            groups: groups,
                            busyVisitId: _busyVisitId,
                            onAction: _act,
                          )
                        : _Timeline(shopId: shopId!, groups: groups);
                  },
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

void _openGroup(BuildContext context, DayGroup group) =>
    context.push('/visits/${group.first.visitId}');

class _Empty extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Empty();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return const Center(
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Text(AppStrings.dayEmpty, style: AppTypography.body),
          SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),
          Text(AppStrings.dayEmptyCaption, style: AppTypography.caption),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _DayList extends StatelessWidget {
  const _DayList({
    required this.groups,
    required this.busyVisitId,
    required this.onAction,
  });

  final List<DayGroup> groups;
  final int? busyVisitId;
  final void Function(DayGroup group, String action) onAction;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final gaps = gapsBefore(groups);
    // 아래 여백은 떠 있는 + 버튼 몫이다.
    return ListView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(AppSpacing.lg, 0, AppSpacing.lg, 120),
      children: [
        for (var i = 0; i < groups.length; i++) ...[
          if (gaps[i] != null)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.sm),
              child: Center(
                child: Text(
                  AppStrings.gapText(gaps[i]!),
                  style: AppTypography.caption,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.sm),
            child: _GroupCard(
              group: groups[i],
              busy: busyVisitId == groups[i].first.visitId,
              otherBusy:
                  busyVisitId != null && busyVisitId != groups[i].first.visitId,
              onAction: (action) => onAction(groups[i], action),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// 목록 카드 — 시간·끝 시각, 사진 자리(가족은 겹친 자리), 이름, 메뉴·보호자,
/// 겹침·정리 안 됨 표시, 상태 버튼.
class _GroupCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _GroupCard({
    required this.group,
    required this.busy,
    required this.otherBusy,
    required this.onAction,
  });

  final DayGroup group;
  final bool busy;
  final bool otherBusy;
  final ValueChanged<String> onAction;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final first = group.first;
    final marks = [
      if (group.overlapping) AppStrings.markOverlap,
      if (group.unresolved) AppStrings.markUnresolved,
    ];
    final status = group.uniformStatus;
    VoidCallback? on(String action) =>
        busy || otherBusy ? null : () => onAction(action);
    final Widget trailing = switch (status) {
      VisitStatus.reserved => AppButton(
        label: AppStrings.actionStart,
        filled: true,
        busy: busy,
        onPressed: on(VisitAction.start),
      ),
      VisitStatus.inProgress => AppButton(
        label: AppStrings.actionCompleteShort,
        filled: true,
        busy: busy,
        onPressed: on(VisitAction.complete),
      ),
      _ => AppChip(label: statusLabel(status ?? first.status)),
    };
    return Opacity(
      opacity: group.occupiesTime ? 1 : 0.5,
      child: Pressable(
        semanticLabel: visitsTitle(group.visits),
        onTap: () => _openGroup(context, group),
        child: AppCard(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppSpacing.md),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              SizedBox(
                width: 48,
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(hhmm(first.startsAt.wall), style: AppTypography.body),
                    Text(
                      '~${hhmm(group.endsAt)}',
                      style: AppTypography.caption,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              _PhotoStack(count: group.items.length),
              const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.md),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      visitsTitle(group.visits),
                      style: AppTypography.bodyStrong,
                    ),
                    Text(
                      [
                        menusLabel(first.menus),
                        ownerLabel(first.customer),
                        ...marks,
                      ].join(' · '),
                      style: AppTypography.caption,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              trailing,
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 사진 자리. 가족이면 두 장을 겹쳐 보인다(사진은 ④에서).
class _PhotoStack extends StatelessWidget {
  const _PhotoStack({required this.count});

  static const size = 38.0;

  final int count;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final shown = count > 1 ? 2 : 1;
    final offset = size * 0.3;
    return SizedBox(
      width: size + offset * (shown - 1),
      height: size,
      child: Stack(
        children: [
          for (var i = 0; i < shown; i++)
            Positioned(
              left: offset * i,
              child: AppPhotoTile(image: null, size: size),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 시간표 — 시간 줄 위에 묶음 블록. 겹치면 옆 칸으로 나눠 그린다. 취소·노쇼는
/// 빈 시간이라 그리지 않는다.
class _Timeline extends ConsumerWidget {
  const _Timeline({required this.shopId, required this.groups});

  final int shopId;
  final List<DayGroup> groups;

  static const _hourHeight = 64.0;
  static const _labelWidth = 44.0;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final settings = ref.watch(visitSettingsProvider(shopId)).value;
    final active = [
      for (final g in groups)
        if (g.occupiesTime) g,
    ];
    // 영업시간을 기본으로, 그 밖의 방문이 있으면 넓힌다.
    var firstHour = settings == null
        ? 10
        : int.parse(settings.opensAt.substring(0, 2));
    var lastHour = settings == null
        ? 19
        : int.parse(settings.closesAt.substring(0, 2));
    for (final g in active) {
      if (g.startsAt.wall.hour < firstHour) firstHour = g.startsAt.wall.hour;
      final end = g.endsAt;
      final endHour = end.minute == 0 ? end.hour : end.hour + 1;
      if (endHour > lastHour) lastHour = endHour;
    }
    final columns = timelineColumns(active);
    final columnCount = columns.isEmpty
        ? 1
        : columns.reduce((a, b) => a > b ? a : b) + 1;
    double top(DateTime t) =>
        ((t.hour - firstHour) * 60 + t.minute) / 60 * _hourHeight;

    return ListView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(AppSpacing.lg, 0, AppSpacing.lg, 120),
      children: [
        LayoutBuilder(
          builder: (context, constraints) {
            final width = (constraints.maxWidth - _labelWidth) / columnCount;
            return SizedBox(
              height: (lastHour - firstHour) * _hourHeight + AppSpacing.md,
              child: Stack(
                children: [
                  for (var h = firstHour; h <= lastHour; h++)
                    Positioned(
                      top: (h - firstHour) * _hourHeight,
                      left: 0,
                      right: 0,
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          SizedBox(
                            width: _labelWidth,
                            child: Text(
                              '${h.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:00',
                              style: AppTypography.caption,
                            ),
                          ),
                          Expanded(
                            child: Container(height: 1, color: AppColors.line),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  for (var i = 0; i < active.length; i++)
                    Positioned(
                      top: top(active[i].startsAt.wall),
                      height:
                          top(active[i].endsAt) - top(active[i].startsAt.wall),
                      left: _labelWidth + width * columns[i],
                      width: width,
                      child: _Block(group: active[i]),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          },
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _Block extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Block({required this.group});

  final DayGroup group;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final inProgress = group.uniformStatus == VisitStatus.inProgress;
    final completed = group.uniformStatus == VisitStatus.completed;
    return Pressable(
      semanticLabel: visitsTitle(group.visits),
      onTap: () => _openGroup(context, group),
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 2, bottom: 2),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          horizontal: AppSpacing.sm,
          vertical: AppSpacing.xs,
        ),
        clipBehavior: Clip.hardEdge,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: completed ? AppColors.backgroundTop : AppColors.white,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadius.sm),
          border: Border.all(
            color: inProgress ? AppColors.ink : AppColors.line,
            width: inProgress ? 1.5 : 1,
          ),
          boxShadow: completed ? null : AppShadows.soft,
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              visitsTitle(group.visits),
              style: AppTypography.bodyStrong,
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            ),
            Text(
              [
                menusLabel(group.first.menus),
                ownerLabel(group.first.customer),
                statusLabel(group.uniformStatus ?? group.first.status),
                if (group.overlapping) AppStrings.markOverlap,
              ].join(' · '),
              style: AppTypography.caption,
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

`app/lib/features/visit/state/day_layout.dart`:

```dart
import '../api/visit_models.dart';

/// 하루 목록의 가족 묶음 하나 — 같은 보호자·같은 시작 시각의 방문들(서버의
/// `group_key`가 같은 줄). 화면은 묶음을 카드 하나로 그린다.
class DayGroup {
  DayGroup(this.items);

  final List<DayVisitItem> items;

  List<Visit> get visits => [for (final i in items) i.visit];
  List<int> get ids => [for (final i in items) i.visit.visitId];
  Visit get first => items.first.visit;
  ShopTime get startsAt => first.startsAt;

  /// 묶음에서 가장 늦게 끝나는 시각.
  DateTime get endsAt => items
      .map((i) => i.visit.endsAt.wall)
      .reduce((a, b) => a.isAfter(b) ? a : b);

  bool get overlapping => items.any((i) => i.overlapping);
  bool get unresolved => items.any((i) => i.unresolved);

  /// 시간을 차지하는 방문이 있는가(취소·노쇼만이면 빈 시간이다).
  bool get occupiesTime => items.any((i) => i.visit.occupiesTime);

  /// 묶음의 상태가 모두 같으면 그 상태, 섞였으면 null.
  String? get uniformStatus {
    final statuses = {for (final i in items) i.visit.status};
    return statuses.length == 1 ? statuses.single : null;
  }
}

/// 같은 `group_key`끼리 묶는다. 처음 나온 순서(=서버의 시작 순)를 지킨다 —
/// 같은 시각에 다른 보호자의 방문이 끼어 있어도 묶음이 쪼개지지 않는다.
List<DayGroup> groupDay(List<DayVisitItem> items) {
  final byKey = <String, List<DayVisitItem>>{};
  for (final item in items) {
    byKey.putIfAbsent(item.groupKey, () => []).add(item);
  }
  return [for (final list in byKey.values) DayGroup(list)];
}

/// 목록에서 [groups]의 각 묶음 앞에 둘 빈 시간(분). 시간을 차지하는 묶음들
/// 사이가 [minMinutes] 이상 비었을 때만 값이 있다. 첫 묶음 앞은 비워 둔다.
List<int?> gapsBefore(List<DayGroup> groups, {int minMinutes = 60}) {
  DateTime? latestEnd;
  return [
    for (final g in groups)
      () {
        if (!g.occupiesTime) return null;
        final start = g.startsAt.wall;
        final gap = latestEnd == null
            ? null
            : start.difference(latestEnd!).inMinutes;
        if (latestEnd == null || g.endsAt.isAfter(latestEnd!)) {
          latestEnd = g.endsAt;
        }
        return gap != null && gap >= minMinutes ? gap : null;
      }(),
  ];
}

/// 시간표의 칸 번호. 앞선 묶음과 겹치면 옆 칸으로 보낸다(가장 왼쪽 빈 칸).
/// [groups]는 시작 순이다. 돌려주는 목록은 같은 순서의 칸 번호다.
List<int> timelineColumns(List<DayGroup> groups) {
  final columnEnds = <DateTime>[];
  final result = <int>[];
  for (final g in groups) {
    final start = g.startsAt.wall;
    var column = columnEnds.indexWhere((end) => !end.isAfter(start));
    if (column == -1) {
      columnEnds.add(g.endsAt);
      column = columnEnds.length - 1;
    } else {
      columnEnds[column] = g.endsAt;
    }
    result.add(column);
  }
  return result;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 골든을 만든다**

Run (`app/`): `flutter test test/features/visit/screens/day_screen_test.dart --update-goldens --timeout 20s`
Expected: 골든 파일이 생긴다(`day_list.png`, `day_timeline.png`). 눈으로 열어 목업과 견준다. **기존 골든이 이 작업의 의도 밖에서 바뀌면 멈추고 보고한다.**

- [ ] **Step 5: 테스트가 통과하는지 확인한다**

Run (`app/`): `flutter test test/features/visit/screens/day_screen_test.dart test/features/visit/state/day_layout_test.dart --timeout 20s`
Expected: 22개 통과(배치 6, 화면 16 — 골든 2 포함)

Run (`app/`): `flutter analyze` → `No issues found!`, `flutter test --timeout 30s` → 전부 통과(434개).

- [ ] **Step 6: 판별력을 확인한다(변이)**

각 변이를 하나씩 넣고 그 테스트가 실패하는지 본 뒤 되돌린다.

- `_GroupCard`의 `busy || otherBusy`를 `busy`로 → `한 카드를 보내는 동안 다른 카드의 버튼도 막는다` 실패.
- `_Timeline`의 `if (g.occupiesTime)` 거르기를 뺀다 → `시간표로 바꾸면…(취소는 그리지 않는다)` 실패.
- `gapsBefore`의 `if (!g.occupiesTime) return null;`을 뺀다 → `취소·노쇼는 빈 시간으로 센다` 실패.
- `timelineColumns`에서 빈 칸 찾기를 뺀다(항상 새 칸) → `겹치는 묶음은 옆 칸으로, 끝난 칸은 다시 쓴다` 실패.
- `_act`의 `ids: group.ids`를 첫 방문만으로 → `카드의 [시작]은 묶음 전체를…`·`카드의 [완료하기]는…` 실패.
- 머리의 `if (isOwner)`를 뺀다 → `직원에게는 ··· 가 없다(V8)` 실패.
- `_openMore`에서 `data.closedDay` 분기를 뺀다(늘 추가) → `사장은 ··· 로 이 날을 휴무로 표시하고 해제한다` 실패(DELETE 가 안 나간다).

- [ ] **Step 7: 커밋한다**

```bash
git add -A
git commit -m "feat(visit): 하루 화면 — 목록·시간표 전환, 묶음 카드의 상태 버튼

Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

---

### Task 8: 캘린더 탭과 홈 셸

`CalendarTab`(월 머리·달력·"정리 안 된 예약 N건"·미리보기 글래스 카드 — 보고 있는 달은 고른 날에서 계산한다), 고른 날 `selectedDateProvider`, 새 디자인 위젯 `AppDashedTile`, `AppBigDate.monthName`. 정리 안 된 예약이 있는 칸은 그 수를 오른쪽 위에 적는다. 홈 셸은 캘린더 탭을 본문으로 쓰고 자기 머리를 매출 탭에만 보이며, 캘린더의 + 는 고른 날로 예약 등록을 열고 저장한 날을 고른다(새로 정한 것 14·17·18, A6).

**기존 테스트 손질:** 홈이 캘린더를 부르게 되어, 홈에서 시작하는 기존 테스트 4개(초대 코드 1, 반려동물 상세 2, 고객 목록 1)가 "첫 요청"을 세거나 모든 요청에 같은 응답을 주던 것을 자기 경로만 보게 좁힌다. 홈 셸 테스트와 골든 테스트는 캘린더 탭 기준으로 다시 쓴다(`home_shell.png` 갱신은 **의도한 변경**이다).

- Create: `app/lib/design/widgets/app_dashed_tile.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3 의 `calendarMonthProvider`·`dayVisitsProvider`, Task 7 의 `groupDay`·`DayScreen`, Task 4 의 `RegisterVisitScreen`·라벨 도우미, `AppGlassCard`·`AppPhotoTile`·`AppBigDate`.
- Produces: `CalendarTab`, `selectedDateProvider`(`SelectedDate.select(date)`), `AppDashedTile(count, size)`, `AppBigDate.monthName(int)`, 문구(달력 묶음 — `unresolvedBanner`·`dayHeading`·`previewCounts`·`calendarWeekHeader`·`weekdaysLong` 등). `AppStrings.calendarEmptyTitle/Caption`은 지운다.

아래 테스트 파일을 만든다. **홈 셸 테스트 둘은 통째로 다시 쓴다**(diff 가 아니라 아래 내용 전체로 바꾼다). 초대·반려동물 상세·고객 목록 테스트는 diff 대로 자기 경로만 보게 좁힌다.

`app/test/features/visit/screens/calendar_tab_test.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:chagocnote/core/router/screen_keys.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/design/widgets/app_button.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/design/widgets/app_dashed_tile.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/design/widgets/app_photo_tile.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/features/home/state/today_provider.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/features/visit/state/calendar_selection.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/l10n/app_strings.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import '../../../support/customer_test_app.dart';
import '../../../support/fake_http_client_adapter.dart';
import '../../../support/visit_fixtures.dart';
import '../../../support/visit_test_backend.dart';

final _today = [todayProvider.overrideWithValue(DateTime(2026, 9, 22))];

/// 9월 — 월요일 휴무, 15일(지난날) 3마리, 22일(오늘) 5마리, 25일(앞날) 예약 4,
/// 정리 안 된 예약 2건(가장 오래된 날 8월 30일).
VisitTestBackend _backend() => VisitTestBackend({
  'GET /v1/shops/3/visits/calendar': (o, b) {
    final month = o.queryParameters['month'] as String;
    if (month != '2026-09') {
      final parts = month.split('-').map(int.parse).toList();
      return FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: calendarJson(year: parts[0], month: parts[1]),
      );
    }
    return FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 200,
      data: calendarJson(
        days: {
          15: {'visit_count': 3, 'completed_count': 3},
          22: {'visit_count': 5, 'completed_count': 1, 'open_count': 4},
          25: {'visit_count': 4, 'open_count': 4},
          30: {'visit_count': 1, 'open_count': 1, 'unresolved_count': 1},
        },
        closedDays: {7, 14, 21, 28},
        unresolvedTotal: 2,
        oldestUnresolvedDate: '2026-08-30',
      ),
    );
  },
  'GET /v1/shops/3/visits': (o, b) {
    final date = o.queryParameters['date'] as String;
    return FakeResponse(
      statusCode: 200,
      data: dayJson(
        date: date,
        items: date == '2026-09-22'
            ? [
                dayItemJson(
                  visitJson(date: date, start: '10:00', status: 'completed'),
                ),
                dayItemJson(
                  visitJson(
                    visitId: 102,
                    date: date,
                    start: '13:00',
                    pet: visitPetJson(petId: 12, name: '보리'),
                  ),
                ),
              ]
            : date == '2026-09-25'
            ? [
                dayItemJson(
                  visitJson(
                    date: date,
                    start: '11:00',
                    pet: visitPetJson(name: '몽이'),
                  ),
                ),
              ]
            : [],
      ),
    );
  },
});

Future<void> _pumpHome(WidgetTester tester, CustomerTestSetup setup) async {
  tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(390, 844);
  tester.view.devicePixelRatio = 1.0;
  addTearDown(tester.view.resetPhysicalSize);
  addTearDown(tester.view.resetDevicePixelRatio);
  await tester.pumpWidget(customerRouterApp(setup.container));
  await settle(tester);
}

Finder _button(String label) =>
    find.byWidgetPredicate((w) => w is AppButton && w.label == label);

void main() {
  testWidgets('지난날은 사진 자리 + 마릿수, 앞날은 점선 칸 + 예약 수, 휴무는 옅은 글자로 보인다', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _pumpHome(tester, setup);

    expect(find.text('09'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('September'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('2026'), findsOneWidget);
    // 지난날 15일과 오늘 22일은 사진 자리, 앞날 25·30일은 점선 칸.
    expect(find.byType(AppPhotoTile), findsNWidgets(2));
    expect(find.byType(AppDashedTile), findsNWidgets(2));
    expect(
      find.descendant(of: find.byType(AppPhotoTile), matching: find.text('5')),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(
      find.descendant(of: find.byType(AppDashedTile), matching: find.text('4')),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.closedShort), findsNWidgets(4));
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.unresolvedBanner(2)), findsOneWidget);
    // '1'은 셋 — 1일 날짜 숫자, 30일 점선 타일의 예약 수, 30일 칸의 정리 안 된 예약 수.
    expect(find.text('1'), findsNWidgets(3));
  });

  testWidgets('오늘이 골라져 있고 미리보기에 그날 줄이 보이며, 다른 날을 누르면 그날을 읽는다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _pumpHome(tester, setup);

    expect(find.text(AppStrings.dayHeading(22, 2)), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.previewCounts(2, 1)), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('두부'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('보리'), findsOneWidget);

    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel('9월 25일 금'));
    await settle(tester);

    expect(setup.container.read(selectedDateProvider), DateTime(2026, 9, 25));
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.dayHeading(25, 5)), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('몽이'), findsOneWidget);
    final dayQueries = [
      for (final r in setup.adapter.requests)
        if (r.path == '/v1/shops/3/visits') r.queryParameters['date'],
    ];
    expect(dayQueries, ['2026-09-22', '2026-09-25']);
  });

  testWidgets('예약이 없는 날과 휴무일은 미리보기에 그렇게 적는다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _pumpHome(tester, setup);

    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel('9월 23일 수'));
    await settle(tester);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.dayEmpty), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('화살표로 달을 옮기면 그달을 읽고, 오늘이 없는 달은 1일을 고른다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _pumpHome(tester, setup);

    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.nextMonth));
    await settle(tester);

    expect(find.text('October'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(setup.container.read(selectedDateProvider), DateTime(2026, 10, 1));
    final months = [
      for (final r in setup.adapter.requests)
        if (r.path.endsWith('/calendar')) r.queryParameters['month'],
    ];
    expect(months, ['2026-09', '2026-10']);

    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.prevMonth));
    await settle(tester);
    expect(find.text('September'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(setup.container.read(selectedDateProvider), DateTime(2026, 9, 22));
  });

  testWidgets('"정리 안 된 예약 N건"을 누르면 가장 오래된 날의 하루 화면으로 간다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _pumpHome(tester, setup);

    await tester.tap(find.text(AppStrings.unresolvedBanner(2)));
    await settle(tester);

    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.day), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('30'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.yearMonth(2026, 8)), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('미리보기를 누르면 고른 날의 하루 화면으로 간다', (tester) async {
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(_backend().call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _pumpHome(tester, setup);

    await tester.tap(find.text(AppStrings.dayHeading(22, 2)));
    await settle(tester);

    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.day), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('22'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('월 요약을 불러오지 못하면 다시 시도한다', (tester) async {
    var fail = true;
    final backend = _backend();
    final ok = backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits/calendar']!;
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visits/calendar'] = (o, b) =>
        fail ? const FakeResponse(statusCode: 503, data: 'x') : ok(o, b);
    final setup = CustomerTestSetup(backend.call, overrides: _today);
    addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
    await _pumpHome(tester, setup);
    expect(_button(AppStrings.retry), findsOneWidget);

    fail = false;
    await tester.tap(_button(AppStrings.retry));
    await settle(tester);

    expect(find.byType(AppPhotoTile), findsNWidgets(2));
  });
}
```

`app/lib/design/widgets/app_dashed_tile.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';

import '../tokens/app_colors.dart';
import '../tokens/app_radius.dart';
import '../tokens/app_typography.dart';

/// 점선 테두리의 둥근 사각형 자리 — 아직 오지 않은 날의 예약처럼 "비어 있지만
/// 잡혀 있는" 것을 보인다. [count]를 가운데에 옅게 적는다.
class AppDashedTile extends StatelessWidget {
  const AppDashedTile({super.key, required this.count, this.size = 38});

  final int count;
  final double size;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: size,
      height: size,
      child: CustomPaint(
        painter: const _DashedBorderPainter(),
        child: Center(
          child: Text(
            '$count',
            style: AppTypography.caption.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _DashedBorderPainter extends CustomPainter {
  const _DashedBorderPainter();

  static const _dash = 3.0;
  static const _gap = 2.5;

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final rect = RRect.fromRectAndRadius(
      Offset.zero & size,
      const Radius.circular(AppRadius.sm),
    ).deflate(0.5);
    canvas.drawRRect(rect, Paint()..color = AppColors.backgroundBottom);
    final border = Paint()
      ..color = AppColors.secondary
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = 1;
    final path = Path()..addRRect(rect);
    for (final metric in path.computeMetrics()) {
      for (var d = 0.0; d < metric.length; d += _dash + _gap) {
        canvas.drawPath(metric.extractPath(d, d + _dash), border);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_DashedBorderPainter oldDelegate) => false;
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../../core/error/error_messages.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_radius.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_spacing.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_typography.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_big_date.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_button.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_card.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_dashed_tile.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_glass_card.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_photo_tile.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/pressable.dart';
import '../../../l10n/app_strings.dart';
import '../../auth/state/session.dart';
import '../../auth/state/session_controller.dart';
import '../../home/state/today_provider.dart';
import '../api/visit_models.dart';
import '../state/calendar_selection.dart';
import '../state/day_layout.dart';
import '../state/visit_providers.dart';
import 'visit_labels.dart';

/// 홈의 "캘린더" 탭 — 큰 세리프 월·연도와 이동 화살표, 월 달력, 위쪽 "정리 안 된
/// 예약 N건" 줄, 고른 날의 미리보기 글래스 카드(설계 9.1).
///
/// 사진이 생기기 전(④ 전)에는 지난날 칸을 회색 사진 자리 + 마릿수로, 앞날의
/// 예약은 점선 칸 + 예약 수로, 휴무일은 옅은 "휴무"로 보인다.
class CalendarTab extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const CalendarTab({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<CalendarTab> createState() => _CalendarTabState();
}

class _CalendarTabState extends ConsumerState<CalendarTab> {
  /// 보고 있는 달(1일) — 늘 고른 날의 달이다. 다른 화면(예약 저장)이 고른 날을
  /// 다른 달로 옮기면 달력도 따라간다.
  DateTime get _month {
    final selected = ref.watch(selectedDateProvider);
    return DateTime(selected.year, selected.month);
  }

  int? get _shopId {
    final session = ref.read(sessionControllerProvider).value;
    return session is SignedIn ? session.selectedShopId : null;
  }

  /// 달을 옮기면 그달에 오늘이 있으면 오늘을, 없으면 1일을 고른다.
  void _moveMonth(int delta) {
    final current = ref.read(selectedDateProvider);
    final month = DateTime(current.year, current.month + delta);
    final today = ref.read(todayProvider);
    ref
        .read(selectedDateProvider.notifier)
        .select(
          today.year == month.year && today.month == month.month
              ? today
              : month,
        );
  }

  void _openDay(DateTime date) => context.push('/visits/day/${isoDay(date)}');

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    ref.watch(sessionControllerProvider);
    final shopId = _shopId;
    final selected = ref.watch(selectedDateProvider);
    if (shopId == null) return const SizedBox.shrink();
    final monthKey = monthKeyOf(shopId, _month);
    final summary = ref.watch(calendarMonthProvider(monthKey));
    final data = summary.value;

    return Stack(
      children: [
        ListView(
          // 아래 여백은 미리보기 카드와 떠 있는 탭바 몫이다.
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
            AppSpacing.lg,
            AppSpacing.md,
            AppSpacing.lg,
            280,
          ),
          children: [
            _MonthHeader(
              month: _month,
              onPrev: () => _moveMonth(-1),
              onNext: () => _moveMonth(1),
            ),
            if (data != null && data.unresolvedTotal > 0)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: AppSpacing.md),
                child: Pressable(
                  semanticLabel: AppStrings.unresolvedBanner(
                    data.unresolvedTotal,
                  ),
                  onTap: data.oldestUnresolvedDate == null
                      ? null
                      : () => _openDay(data.oldestUnresolvedDate!),
                  child: AppCard(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                      horizontal: AppSpacing.md,
                      vertical: AppSpacing.sm,
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          AppStrings.unresolvedBanner(data.unresolvedTotal),
                          style: AppTypography.bodyStrong,
                        ),
                        const Spacer(),
                        const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size: 18),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),
            const _WeekHeader(),
            const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),
            if (summary.hasError && data == null)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppSpacing.lg),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    Text(messageFor(summary.error!), style: AppTypography.body),
                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),
                    AppButton(
                      label: AppStrings.retry,
                      onPressed: () =>
                          ref.invalidate(calendarMonthProvider(monthKey)),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              )
            else
              _MonthGrid(
                month: _month,
                summary: data,
                today: ref.watch(todayProvider),
                selected: selected,
                onSelect: (date) =>
                    ref.read(selectedDateProvider.notifier).select(date),
              ),
          ],
        ),
        // 오른쪽은 떠 있는 + 버튼 자리다.
        Positioned(
          left: AppSpacing.md,
          right: 76,
          bottom: 96,
          child: _DayPreview(
            shopId: shopId,
            date: selected,
            onOpen: () => _openDay(selected),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// "09 September 2026 ‹ ›" 와 설정 버튼.
class _MonthHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  const _MonthHeader({
    required this.month,
    required this.onPrev,
    required this.onNext,
  });

  final DateTime month;
  final VoidCallback onPrev;
  final VoidCallback onNext;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final monthName = AppBigDate.monthName(month.month);
    return Row(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
      children: [
        Text(
          month.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0'),
          style: AppTypography.bigNumeral,
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),
        Expanded(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.xs),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text('${month.year}', style: AppTypography.caption),
                Text(
                  monthName,
                  style: AppTypography.italicLabel.copyWith(fontSize: 24),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        Pressable(
          semanticLabel: AppStrings.prevMonth,
          onTap: onPrev,
          child: const Icon(Icons.chevron_left),
        ),
        Pressable(
          semanticLabel: AppStrings.nextMonth,
          onTap: onNext,
          child: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
        ),
        Pressable(
          semanticLabel: AppStrings.settingsLabel,
          onTap: () => context.push('/settings'),
          child: const Icon(Icons.settings_outlined),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _WeekHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  const _WeekHeader();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        for (final d in AppStrings.calendarWeekHeader)
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              d,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: AppTypography.caption,
            ),
          ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// 일요일부터 시작하는 월 달력(기준 목업).
class _MonthGrid extends StatelessWidget {
  const _MonthGrid({
    required this.month,
    required this.summary,
    required this.today,
    required this.selected,
    required this.onSelect,
  });

  final DateTime month;
  final CalendarMonth? summary;
  final DateTime today;
  final DateTime selected;
  final ValueChanged<DateTime> onSelect;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final lead = DateTime(month.year, month.month, 1).weekday % 7;
    final days = DateTime(month.year, month.month + 1, 0).day;
    final cells = lead + days;
    final rows = (cells / 7).ceil();
    final todayDate = DateTime(today.year, today.month, today.day);
    return Column(
      children: [
        for (var r = 0; r < rows; r++)
          Row(
            children: [
              for (var c = 0; c < 7; c++)
                Expanded(
                  child: () {
                    final day = r * 7 + c - lead + 1;
                    if (day < 1 || day > days) {
                      return const SizedBox(height: 56);
                    }
                    final date = DateTime(month.year, month.month, day);
                    return _DayCell(
                      date: date,
                      summary: summary?.dayOf(date),
                      isToday: date == todayDate,
                      isPast: date.isBefore(todayDate),
                      isSelected: date == selected,
                      onTap: () => onSelect(date),
                    );
                  }(),
                ),
            ],
          ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _DayCell extends StatelessWidget {
  const _DayCell({
    required this.date,
    required this.summary,
    required this.isToday,
    required this.isPast,
    required this.isSelected,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  final DateTime date;
  final CalendarDay? summary;
  final bool isToday;
  final bool isPast;
  final bool isSelected;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = summary;
    final count = s?.visitCount ?? 0;
    final Widget body;
    if (count > 0 && (isPast || isToday)) {
      body = AppPhotoTile(image: null, count: count, size: 34);
    } else if (count > 0) {
      body = AppDashedTile(count: count, size: 34);
    } else if (s?.closedDay ?? false) {
      body = Text(
        AppStrings.closedShort,
        style: AppTypography.caption.copyWith(
          color: AppColors.line,
          fontSize: 9,
        ),
      );
    } else {
      body = const SizedBox.shrink();
    }
    return Pressable(
      semanticLabel: dayLabel(date),
      selected: isSelected,
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Container(
        height: 56,
        margin: const EdgeInsets.all(1),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadius.sm),
          border: Border.all(
            color: isSelected ? AppColors.ink : const Color(0x00000000),
          ),
        ),
        child: ExcludeSemantics(
          child: Stack(
            children: [
              Positioned(left: 4, top: 2, child: _DayNumber(date, isToday)),
              // 정리 안 된 예약 수(설계 6.4 "날마다 수를").
              if ((s?.unresolvedCount ?? 0) > 0)
                Positioned(
                  right: 3,
                  top: 1,
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 3),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppColors.ink,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadius.pill),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      '${s!.unresolvedCount}',
                      style: AppTypography.caption.copyWith(
                        fontSize: 8,
                        height: 1.4,
                        color: AppColors.white,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              Positioned(
                left: 0,
                right: 0,
                top: 17,
                bottom: 2,
                child: Center(child: body),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _DayNumber extends StatelessWidget {
  const _DayNumber(this.date, this.isToday);

  final DateTime date;
  final bool isToday;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final text = Text(
      '${date.day}',
      style: AppTypography.caption.copyWith(
        fontSize: 10,
        color: isToday ? AppColors.white : AppColors.ink,
        fontWeight: isToday ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w500,
      ),
    );
    if (!isToday) return text;
    return Container(
      width: 16,
      height: 16,
      alignment: Alignment.center,
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.ink,
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
      ),
      child: text,
    );
  }
}

/// 고른 날의 미리보기 — 떠 있는 글래스 카드. 누르면 하루 화면.
class _DayPreview extends ConsumerWidget {
  const _DayPreview({
    required this.shopId,
    required this.date,
    required this.onOpen,
  });

  final int shopId;
  final DateTime date;
  final VoidCallback onOpen;

  static const _maxRows = 4;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final day = ref.watch(dayVisitsProvider((shopId: shopId, date: date)));
    final data = day.value;
    final groups = data == null ? const <DayGroup>[] : groupDay(data.items);
    return Pressable(
      semanticLabel: AppStrings.dayHeading(date.day, date.weekday),
      onTap: onOpen,
      child: AppGlassCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
          AppSpacing.lg,
          AppSpacing.md,
          AppSpacing.lg,
          AppSpacing.md,
        ),
        child: ExcludeSemantics(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              Row(
                children: [
                  Text(
                    AppStrings.dayHeading(date.day, date.weekday),
                    style: AppTypography.bodyStrong,
                  ),
                  const Spacer(),
                  if (data != null)
                    Text(
                      AppStrings.previewCounts(
                        data.counts.visitCount,
                        data.counts.completed,
                      ),
                      style: AppTypography.caption,
                    ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),
              if (day.hasError)
                Text(messageFor(day.error!), style: AppTypography.caption)
              else if (data != null && groups.isEmpty)
                Text(
                  data.closedDay
                      ? AppStrings.closedDayNote
                      : AppStrings.dayEmpty,
                  style: AppTypography.caption,
                )
              else
                for (final g in groups.take(_maxRows))
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 2),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        SizedBox(
                          width: 44,
                          child: Text(
                            hhmm(g.startsAt.wall),
                            style: AppTypography.caption,
                          ),
                        ),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Text(
                            visitsTitle(g.visits),
                            style: AppTypography.body,
                            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          ),
                        ),
                        Text(
                          [
                            statusLabel(g.uniformStatus ?? g.first.status),
                            if (g.overlapping) AppStrings.markOverlap,
                          ].join(' · '),
                          style: AppTypography.caption,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../home/state/today_provider.dart';

/// 캘린더 탭에서 고른 날. 처음에는 오늘이다. 홈의 + 버튼이 이 날로 예약
/// 등록을 연다(달력에서 날짜를 고른 뒤 +, 설계 9.1).
class SelectedDate extends Notifier<DateTime> {
  @override
  DateTime build() {
    final today = ref.watch(todayProvider);
    return DateTime(today.year, today.month, today.day);
  }

  void select(DateTime date) =>
      state = DateTime(date.year, date.month, date.day);
}

final selectedDateProvider = NotifierProvider<SelectedDate, DateTime>(
  SelectedDate.new,
);
```

```diff
-          Text(title, style: AppTypography.body, textAlign: TextAlign.center),
-          if (caption != null) ...[

-            Text(
-              caption!,
-              style: AppTypography.caption,
-              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
-            ),
-          ],
-        ],
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 골든을 만든다**

Run (`app/`): `flutter test test/design/app_dashed_tile_golden_test.dart test/features/home/screens/home_shell_golden_test.dart --update-goldens --timeout 20s`
Expected: 골든 파일이 생긴다(`app_dashed_tile.png`, `home_shell.png`). 눈으로 열어 목업과 견준다. **기존 골든이 이 작업의 의도 밖에서 바뀌면 멈추고 보고한다.**

- [ ] **Step 5: 테스트가 통과하는지 확인한다**

Run (`app/`): `flutter test test/features/visit/screens/calendar_tab_test.dart test/features/home test/design/app_dashed_tile_golden_test.dart --timeout 20s`
Expected: 14개 통과(캘린더 7, 홈 셸 5, 홈 골든 1, 점선 타일 골든 1)

Run (`app/`): `flutter analyze` → `No issues found!`, `flutter test --timeout 30s` → 전부 통과(443개).

- [ ] **Step 6: 판별력을 확인한다(변이)**

각 변이를 하나씩 넣고 그 테스트가 실패하는지 본 뒤 되돌린다.

- `_DayCell`의 `(isPast || isToday)` 조건을 뺀다(모두 사진 자리) → `지난날은 사진 자리 + 마릿수, 앞날은 점선 칸…`·`월 요약을 불러오지 못하면…` 실패.
- `_moveMonth`가 늘 1일을 고르게 → `화살표로 달을 옮기면…` 실패.
- "정리 안 된 예약" 카드가 고른 날로 가게 → `"정리 안 된 예약 N건"을 누르면…` 실패.
- 홈 셸 `_reserve`에서 저장한 날 고르기를 뺀다 → `캘린더 탭의 + 는 고른 날로 예약 등록을 열고, 저장한 날을 고른다` 실패.
- `_month` 를 고른 날에서 계산하지 않고 처음 달로 고정한다 → `캘린더 탭의 + 는…` 실패(October 가 안 보인다).
- 정리 안 됨 수를 그리지 않는다 → `지난날은 사진 자리…` 실패('1' 이 둘).

- [ ] **Step 7: 커밋한다**

```bash
git add -A
git commit -m "feat(visit): 캘린더 탭 — 월 달력, 정리 안 된 예약, 미리보기 카드, 홈 셸 연결

Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

### Task 9: 설정 → 일정(사장만)

`ScheduleSettingsScreen`(`/settings/schedule`, `lib/features/shop`) — 영업시간(시·분 칩 시트)·기본 소요·쉬는 요일을 [저장]으로, 임시 휴무는 줄마다 바로. 입력 규칙은 `schedule_input.dart`. 설정 허브에 "일정" 행을 사장에게만 더한다(새로 정한 것 15, V8·A7).

**Files:**
- Modify: `app/lib/core/router/app_router.dart`
- Modify: `app/lib/core/router/screen_keys.dart`
- Create: `app/lib/features/shop/screens/schedule_settings_screen.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3 의 `visitSettingsProvider`·`closedDatesProvider`·`VisitCommands.updateSettings/addClosedDate/deleteClosedDate`, Task 4 의 `stepDuration`·`dayLabel`, `AppChoiceChip`·`AppSheet`.
- Produces: `ScheduleSettingsScreen`, `ScreenKeys.settingsSchedule`, 라우트 `/settings/schedule`(`_shopScopedSettings`에 더함), `minutesOf/hhmmOf/opensBeforeCloses/parseClosedDate`(`schedule_input.dart`), 문구(일정 묶음 — `settingsSchedule`·`settingsMenus` 포함).

`app/test/features/shop/screens/schedule_settings_screen_test.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:chagocnote/core/router/screen_keys.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/design/widgets/app_button.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/design/widgets/app_choice_chip.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/features/home/state/today_provider.dart';
import 'package:chagocnote/l10n/app_strings.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import '../../../support/customer_test_app.dart';
import '../../../support/fake_http_client_adapter.dart';
import '../../../support/visit_fixtures.dart';
import '../../../support/visit_test_backend.dart';

final _today = [todayProvider.overrideWithValue(DateTime(2026, 9, 23))];

VisitTestBackend _backend() => VisitTestBackend({
  'GET /v1/shops/3/closed-dates': (o, b) => FakeResponse(
    statusCode: 200,
    data: {
      'items': [
        {'date': '2026-10-09', 'note': '한글날'},
      ],
    },
  ),
  'PUT /v1/shops/3/visit-settings': (o, b) => FakeResponse(
    statusCode: 200,
    data: settingsJson(opensAt: '09:30:00', closedWeekdays: [0, 6]),
  ),
  'POST /v1/shops/3/closed-dates': (o, b) =>
      FakeResponse(statusCode: 200, data: {'date': '2026-12-25', 'note': null}),
  'DELETE /v1/shops/3/closed-dates/2026-10-09': (o, b) =>
      const FakeResponse(statusCode: 204),
});

Future<CustomerTestSetup> _open(
  WidgetTester tester, {
  VisitTestBackend? backend,
  String role = 'owner',
}) async {
  final setup = CustomerTestSetup(
    (backend ?? _backend()).call,
    role: role,
    overrides: _today,
  );
  addTearDown(setup.container.dispose);
  useTallView(tester);
  await pushOnHome<Object?>(tester, setup, '/settings/schedule');
  return setup;
}

Finder _button(String label) =>
    find.byWidgetPredicate((w) => w is AppButton && w.label == label);

Future<void> _tap(WidgetTester tester, Finder finder) async {
  await tester.tap(finder);
  await settle(tester);
}

void main() {
  testWidgets('영업시간·기본 소요·쉬는 요일·임시 휴무를 서버 값으로 보인다', (tester) async {
    await _open(tester);

    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.settingsSchedule), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('10:00'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('19:00'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.durationText(120)), findsOneWidget);
    final monday = tester.widget<AppChoiceChip>(
      find.byWidgetPredicate((w) => w is AppChoiceChip && w.label == '월'),
    );
    expect(monday.selected, isTrue);
    expect(find.text('10월 9일 금 · 한글날'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('시각을 시트에서 고르고 요일·소요를 바꿔 저장하면 전체를 PUT 으로 보낸다', (tester) async {
    final backend = _backend();
    Map<String, dynamic>? sent;
    backend.routes['PUT /v1/shops/3/visit-settings'] = (o, body) {
      sent = bodyOf(body);
      return FakeResponse(statusCode: 200, data: settingsJson());
    };
    await _open(tester, backend: backend);

    await _tap(tester, find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.opensAtLabel));
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.pickTimeTitle), findsOneWidget);
    await _tap(tester, find.bySemanticsLabel('09'));
    await _tap(tester, find.bySemanticsLabel('30'));
    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.confirm));
    expect(find.text('09:30'), findsOneWidget);
    await _tap(tester, find.bySemanticsLabel('일'));
    // 한 번 누르면 10분, 길게 누르면 30분.
    await _tap(tester, find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.longer));
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.durationText(130)), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.longPress(find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.shorter));
    await settle(tester);
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.durationText(100)), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.longPress(find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.longer));
    await settle(tester);
    await tester.longPress(find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.longer));
    await settle(tester);
    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.save));

    expect(sent, {
      'opens_at': '09:30',
      'closes_at': '19:00',
      'default_duration_minutes': 160,
      'closed_weekdays': [0, 6],
    });
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.saved), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('닫는 시각이 여는 시각보다 빠르면 보내지 않고 알린다', (tester) async {
    final setup = await _open(tester);

    await _tap(tester, find.bySemanticsLabel(AppStrings.closesAtLabel));
    await _tap(tester, find.bySemanticsLabel('09'));
    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.confirm));
    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.save));

    expect(find.text(AppStrings.closesBeforeOpens), findsOneWidget);
    expect(setup.calls, isNot(contains('PUT /v1/shops/3/visit-settings')));
  });

  testWidgets('임시 휴무를 더하고 지운다 — 잘못된 날짜는 보내지 않는다', (tester) async {
    final backend = _backend();
    Map<String, dynamic>? added;
    backend.routes['POST /v1/shops/3/closed-dates'] = (o, body) {
      added = bodyOf(body);
      return FakeResponse(
        statusCode: 200,
        data: {'date': '2026-12-25', 'note': '성탄절'},
      );
    };
    final setup = await _open(tester, backend: backend);

    final fields = find.byType(TextField);
    await tester.enterText(fields.at(0), '2026.09.01');
    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.addClosedDate));
    expect(find.text(AppStrings.closedDateInvalid), findsOneWidget);
    expect(added, isNull);

    await tester.enterText(fields.at(0), '2026.12.25');
    await tester.enterText(fields.at(1), '성탄절');
    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.addClosedDate));
    expect(added, {'date': '2026-12-25', 'note': '성탄절'});
    expect(
      setup.calls.where((c) => c == 'GET /v1/shops/3/closed-dates'),
      hasLength(2),
    );

    await _tap(tester, find.bySemanticsLabel('${AppStrings.delete} 10월 9일 금'));
    expect(setup.calls, contains('DELETE /v1/shops/3/closed-dates/2026-10-09'));
  });

  testWidgets('서버가 거절하면(직원의 403) 문구를 보이고 남는다', (tester) async {
    final backend = _backend();
    backend.routes['PUT /v1/shops/3/visit-settings'] = (o, b) =>
        const FakeResponse(
          statusCode: 403,
          data: {'code': 'PERMISSION_DENIED', 'message': 'x'},
        );
    await _open(tester, backend: backend);

    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.save));

    expect(find.byKey(ScreenKeys.settingsSchedule), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.textContaining('권한이 없습니다'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('설정을 불러오지 못하면 다시 시도한다', (tester) async {
    var fail = true;
    final backend = _backend();
    final ok = backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visit-settings']!;
    backend.routes['GET /v1/shops/3/visit-settings'] = (o, b) =>
        fail ? const FakeResponse(statusCode: 503, data: 'x') : ok(o, b);
    await _open(tester, backend: backend);
    expect(_button(AppStrings.retry), findsOneWidget);

    fail = false;
    await _tap(tester, _button(AppStrings.retry));

    expect(find.text('10:00'), findsOneWidget);
  });
}
```

```dart
import 'package:chagocnote/features/shop/state/schedule_input.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

void main() {
  final today = DateTime(2026, 9, 23);

  test('여는 시각은 닫는 시각보다 빨라야 한다', () {
    expect(opensBeforeCloses('10:00', '19:00'), isTrue);
    expect(opensBeforeCloses('19:00', '19:00'), isFalse);
    expect(opensBeforeCloses('20:00', '19:00'), isFalse);
  });

  test('분 ↔ HH:MM', () {
    expect(minutesOf('09:30'), 570);
    expect(hhmmOf(570), '09:30');
    expect(hhmmOf(0), '00:00');
  });

  test('임시 휴무 날짜는 점·하이픈·붙여쓰기를 받고, 오늘부터만', () {
    expect(parseClosedDate('2026.10.09', today), DateTime(2026, 10, 9));
    expect(parseClosedDate('2026-10-09', today), DateTime(2026, 10, 9));
    expect(parseClosedDate('20261009', today), DateTime(2026, 10, 9));
    expect(parseClosedDate('2026.09.23', today), DateTime(2026, 9, 23));
    expect(parseClosedDate('2026.09.22', today), isNull);
    expect(parseClosedDate('2026.02.30', today), isNull);
    expect(parseClosedDate('10.09', today), isNull);
  });
}
```

`app/lib/features/shop/screens/schedule_settings_screen.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../../core/error/error_messages.dart';
import '../../../core/router/screen_keys.dart';
import '../../../design/app_theme.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_spacing.dart';
import '../../../design/tokens/app_typography.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_back_button.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_button.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_card.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_choice_chip.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/app_sheet.dart';
import '../../../design/widgets/pressable.dart';
import '../../../l10n/app_strings.dart';
import '../../auth/state/session.dart';
import '../../auth/state/session_controller.dart';
import '../../home/state/today_provider.dart';
import '../../visit/api/visit_models.dart';
import '../../visit/screens/visit_labels.dart';
import '../../visit/state/visit_commands.dart';
import '../../visit/state/visit_form_rules.dart';
import '../../visit/state/visit_providers.dart';
import '../state/schedule_input.dart';

/// 설정 → 일정(사장만) — 영업시간, 기본 소요, 매주 쉬는 요일, 임시 휴무
/// (설계 9.1). 영업시간·소요·요일은 [저장]으로 한 번에 보내고, 임시 휴무는
/// 줄마다 바로 더하고 지운다.
class ScheduleSettingsScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const ScheduleSettingsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<ScheduleSettingsScreen> createState() =>
      _ScheduleSettingsScreenState();
}

class _ScheduleSettingsScreenState
    extends ConsumerState<ScheduleSettingsScreen> {
  final _date = TextEditingController();
  final _note = TextEditingController();

  bool _initialized = false;
  late String _opensAt;
  late String _closesAt;
  late int _duration;
  late Set<int> _closedWeekdays;

  bool _busy = false;
  String? _errorText;
  String? _successText;
  String? _dateError;

  int? get _shopId {
    final session = ref.read(sessionControllerProvider).value;
    return session is SignedIn ? session.selectedShopId : null;
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _date.dispose();
    _note.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _init(VisitSettings settings) {
    if (_initialized) return;
    _initialized = true;
    _opensAt = settings.opensAt;
    _closesAt = settings.closesAt;
    _duration = settings.defaultDurationMinutes;
    _closedWeekdays = {...settings.closedWeekdays};
  }

  /// 10분씩, 길게 누르면 30분씩.
  void _stepDuration({required bool longer, bool long = false}) => setState(() {
    _duration = stepDuration(_duration, longer: longer, long: long);
    _successText = null;
  });

  Future<void> _pickTime({required bool opens}) async {
    final picked = await AppSheet.show<String>(
      context,
      builder: (context) => _TimeSheet(initial: opens ? _opensAt : _closesAt),
    );
    if (picked == null || !mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      if (opens) {
        _opensAt = picked;
      } else {
        _closesAt = picked;
      }
      _successText = null;
    });
  }

  Future<void> _save() async {
    final shopId = _shopId;
    if (shopId == null || _busy) return;
    if (!opensBeforeCloses(_opensAt, _closesAt)) {
      setState(() => _errorText = AppStrings.closesBeforeOpens);
      return;
    }
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _errorText = null;
      _successText = null;
    });
    try {
      await ref
          .read(visitCommandsProvider)
          .updateSettings(
            shopId,
            VisitSettings(
              opensAt: _opensAt,
              closesAt: _closesAt,
              defaultDurationMinutes: _duration,
              closedWeekdays: _closedWeekdays.toList()..sort(),
            ),
          );
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _busy = false;
        _successText = AppStrings.saved;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _busy = false;
        _errorText = messageFor(e);
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _addClosedDate() async {
    final shopId = _shopId;
    if (shopId == null || _busy) return;
    final date = parseClosedDate(_date.text, ref.read(todayProvider));
    if (date == null) {
      setState(() => _dateError = AppStrings.closedDateInvalid);
      return;
    }
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _dateError = null;
      _errorText = null;
    });
    try {
      final note = _note.text.trim();
      await ref
          .read(visitCommandsProvider)
          .addClosedDate(shopId, date, note: note.isEmpty ? null : note);
      if (!mounted) return;
      _date.clear();
      _note.clear();
      setState(() => _busy = false);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _busy = false;
        _errorText = messageFor(e);
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _deleteClosedDate(DateTime date) async {
    final shopId = _shopId;
    if (shopId == null || _busy) return;
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _errorText = null;
    });
    try {
      await ref.read(visitCommandsProvider).deleteClosedDate(shopId, date);
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _busy = false;
        _errorText = messageFor(e);
      });
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final shopId = _shopId;
    final settings = shopId == null
        ? const AsyncValue<VisitSettings>.loading()
        : ref.watch(visitSettingsProvider(shopId));
    return Scaffold(
      key: ScreenKeys.settingsSchedule,
      body: AppBackground(
        child: SafeArea(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              const Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                  AppSpacing.lg,
                  AppSpacing.sm,
                  AppSpacing.lg,
                  AppSpacing.sm,
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    AppBackButton(),
                    SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),
                    Text(
                      AppStrings.settingsSchedule,
                      style: AppTypography.titleKo,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Expanded(
                child: settings.when(
                  loading: () =>
                      const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
                  error: (error, _) => Center(
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Text(messageFor(error), style: AppTypography.body),
                        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
                        AppButton(
                          label: AppStrings.retry,
                          onPressed: () =>
                              ref.invalidate(visitSettingsProvider(shopId!)),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  data: (data) {
                    _init(data);
                    return _body(shopId!);
                  },
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _body(int shopId) {
    final today = ref.watch(todayProvider);
    final closedDates = ref.watch(
      closedDatesProvider((
        shopId: shopId,
        from: DateTime(today.year, today.month, today.day),
      )),
    );
    return ListView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
        AppSpacing.lg,
        0,
        AppSpacing.lg,
        AppSpacing.xxl,
      ),
      children: [
        AppCard(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              _TimeRow(
                label: AppStrings.opensAtLabel,
                value: _opensAt,
                onTap: () => _pickTime(opens: true),
              ),
              _TimeRow(
                label: AppStrings.closesAtLabel,
                value: _closesAt,
                onTap: () => _pickTime(opens: false),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),
              Row(
                children: [
                  const Text(
                    AppStrings.defaultDurationLabel,
                    style: AppTypography.body,
                  ),
                  const Spacer(),
                  Pressable(
                    semanticLabel: AppStrings.shorter,
                    onTap: () => _stepDuration(longer: false),
                    onLongPress: () => _stepDuration(longer: false, long: true),
                    child: const Icon(Icons.remove, size: 18),
                  ),
                  Text(
                    AppStrings.durationText(_duration),
                    style: AppTypography.bodyStrong,
                  ),
                  Pressable(
                    semanticLabel: AppStrings.longer,
                    onTap: () => _stepDuration(longer: true),
                    onLongPress: () => _stepDuration(longer: true, long: true),
                    child: const Icon(Icons.add, size: 18),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),
              const Text(
                AppStrings.closedWeekdaysLabel,
                style: AppTypography.caption,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),
              Wrap(
                spacing: AppSpacing.sm,
                runSpacing: AppSpacing.sm,
                children: [
                  for (var d = 0; d < 7; d++)
                    AppChoiceChip(
                      label: AppStrings.weekdaysShort[d],
                      selected: _closedWeekdays.contains(d),
                      onTap: () => setState(() {
                        if (!_closedWeekdays.remove(d)) _closedWeekdays.add(d);
                        _successText = null;
                      }),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
              if (_errorText != null)
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.sm),
                  child: Text(_errorText!, style: AppTypography.caption),
                ),
              if (_successText != null)
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.sm),
                  child: Text(_successText!, style: AppTypography.caption),
                ),
              AppButton(
                label: AppStrings.save,
                filled: true,
                busy: _busy,
                onPressed: _busy ? null : _save,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
        AppCard(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              const Text(
                AppStrings.closedDatesLabel,
                style: AppTypography.caption,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),
              closedDates.when(
                loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
                error: (error, _) => Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        messageFor(error),
                        style: AppTypography.caption,
                      ),
                    ),
                    AppButton(
                      label: AppStrings.retry,
                      onPressed: () => ref.invalidate(closedDatesProvider),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                data: (items) => items.isEmpty
                    ? const Text(
                        AppStrings.closedDatesEmpty,
                        style: AppTypography.body,
                      )
                    : Column(
                        children: [
                          for (final c in items)
                            Row(
                              children: [
                                Expanded(
                                  child: Text(
                                    [
                                      dayLabel(c.date),
                                      if (c.note != null) c.note!,
                                    ].join(' · '),
                                    style: AppTypography.body,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                Pressable(
                                  semanticLabel:
                                      '${AppStrings.delete} ${dayLabel(c.date)}',
                                  onTap: _busy
                                      ? null
                                      : () => _deleteClosedDate(c.date),
                                  child: const Icon(
                                    Icons.close,
                                    size: 18,
                                    color: AppColors.secondary,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                        ],
                      ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),
              TextField(
                controller: _date,
                keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                inputFormatters: [LengthLimitingTextInputFormatter(10)],
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  hintText: AppStrings.closedDateHint,
                  errorText: _dateError,
                ),
              ),
              TextField(
                controller: _note,
                inputFormatters: [LengthLimitingTextInputFormatter(30)],
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  hintText: AppStrings.closedDateNoteHint,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),
              AppButton(
                label: AppStrings.addClosedDate,
                onPressed: _busy ? null : _addClosedDate,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _TimeRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const _TimeRow({
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  final String label;
  final String value;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Pressable(
      semanticLabel: label,
      onTap: onTap,
      // 이름표는 Pressable 이 읽는다 — 글자를 한 번 더 읽지 않게 뺀다.
      child: ExcludeSemantics(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppSpacing.xs),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Text(label, style: AppTypography.body),
              const Spacer(),
              Text(value, style: AppTypography.bodyStrong),
              const Icon(
                Icons.chevron_right,
                size: 18,
                color: AppColors.secondary,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 시(0~23)와 분(10분 단위)을 칩으로 고르는 시트.
class _TimeSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  const _TimeSheet({required this.initial});

  final String initial;

  @override
  State<_TimeSheet> createState() => _TimeSheetState();
}

class _TimeSheetState extends State<_TimeSheet> {
  late int _hour = int.parse(widget.initial.substring(0, 2));
  late int _minute = int.parse(widget.initial.substring(3, 5));

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        const Text(AppStrings.pickTimeTitle, style: AppTypography.body),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),
        const Text(AppStrings.hourLabel, style: AppTypography.caption),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),
        Wrap(
          spacing: AppSpacing.xs,
          runSpacing: AppSpacing.xs,
          children: [
            for (var h = 0; h < 24; h++)
              AppChoiceChip(
                label: h.toString().padLeft(2, '0'),
                selected: h == _hour,
                onTap: () => setState(() => _hour = h),
              ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),
        const Text(AppStrings.minuteLabel, style: AppTypography.caption),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),
        Wrap(
          spacing: AppSpacing.xs,
          runSpacing: AppSpacing.xs,
          children: [
            for (var m = 0; m < 60; m += 10)
              AppChoiceChip(
                label: m.toString().padLeft(2, '0'),
                selected: m == _minute,
                onTap: () => setState(() => _minute = m),
              ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),
        AppButton(
          label: AppStrings.confirm,
          filled: true,
          onPressed: () =>
              Navigator.of(context).pop(hhmmOf(_hour * 60 + _minute)),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```
